# <span class="deva-large">च</span> <span class="iast-large">ca</span>

<div class="dict-page-header">
  <span class="badge-count">443 entrées</span>
  <a href="../" class="back-link">← Index du dictionnaire</a>
</div>

---

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">च</span> <i>ca</i> conj. cl. et, aussi, ainsi que <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mais, par contre, bien que; si <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> même; précisément, uniquement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>que</i>.<br> <i>api</i> <i>ca</i> même.<br> <i>ca</i> <i>...</i> <i>ca</i> et ... et; non seulement ... mais encore; à peine ... que.<br> <i>caiva</i> aussi.<br> <i>tathā</i> <i>ca</i> et.<br> <i>na</i> <i>...</i> <i>ca</i> ni ... ni; et ne ... pas <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>neque</i>.<br> <i>ca</i> <i>...</i> <i>naca</i> toutefois ... néanmoins.<br> <i>naca</i> <i>...</i> <i>ca</i> néanmoins ... pourtant.<br> <i>...</i> <i>varaṃ</i> <i>na</i> <i>ca</i> plutôt ... que; mieux vaut ... que.<br> <i>tava</i> <i>ca</i> <i>mama</i> <i>ca</i> à toi et à moi.<br> <i>na</i> <i>pūrvāhṇe</i> <i>na</i> <i>ca</i> <i>parāhṇe</i> ni le matin ni l'après-midi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चण्ड</span> <i>caṇḍa</i> a. <a href="#entry-ca_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ca_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ca_n_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caṇḍā</i> <a href="#entry-ca_n_dii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caṇḍī</i> chaud, ardent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> emporté, féroce, cruel, furieux, violent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> circoncis <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Caṇḍa «Furieux», l'un des deux gardiens [<a href="#entry-dvaarapaalau" class="skt-link">dvārapālau</a>] de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; son compagnon est <a href="#entry-Upraca_n_da" class="skt-link">Pracaṇḍa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-muni" class="skt-link">muni</a> Caṇḍa_1 «le Terrible» <a href="#entry-Ukauzika" class="skt-link">Kauśika</a>, fils de <a href="#entry-Ukak_siivaan" class="skt-link">Kakṣīvān</a> <a href="#entry-Ugautama" class="skt-link">Gautama</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ub_rhadratha" class="skt-link">Bṛhadratha</a> — f. cf. <a href="#entry-ca_n_dii" class="skt-link">caṇḍī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_n_daala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चण्डाल</span> <i>caṇḍāla</i> var. <i>cāṇḍāla</i> [<a href="#entry-ca_n_da" class="skt-link">caṇḍa</a><a href="#entry--ala" class="skt-link">-ala</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ca_n_daala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ca_n_daala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ca_n_daalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caṇḍālā</i> cruel; sauvage — <a href="#entry-ca_n_daala" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. hors-caste (not. fils d'un serviteur [<a href="#entry-zuudra" class="skt-link">śūdra</a>] et d'une <a href="#entry-braahma_nii" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇī</a>); intouchable s'occupant de la préparation et de l'incinération des morts, ou de l'exécution des criminels.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_n_daalataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caṇḍālatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-ca_n_daalataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. condition de paria.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_n_daalatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caṇḍālatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-ca_n_daalatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. condition de paria.<br> <a href="#entry-ca_n_daala" class="skt-link">caṇḍāla</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_n_dagho_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caṇḍaghoṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-gho_sa" class="skt-link">ghoṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-ca_n_dagho_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. [DKC.] np. du prince Caṇḍaghoṣa «aux cris de fureur», fils de <a href="#entry-Uca_n_dasi_mha" class="skt-link">Caṇḍasiṃha</a> et <a href="#entry-Uliilaavatii_1" class="skt-link">Līlāvatī_1</a>; il mourut d'épuisement de sa vie dissolue; sa femme <a href="#entry-Uaacaaravatii" class="skt-link">Ācāravatī</a> mourut en couches, donnant naissance à <a href="#entry-Uma_nikar_nikaa" class="skt-link">Maṇikarṇikā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_n_dakauzika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caṇḍakauśika</i> [<a href="#entry-kauzika_1" class="skt-link">kauśika_1</a>] <a href="#entry-ca_n_dakauzika" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Caṇḍakauśika «plein de fureur», fils du <a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a> <a href="#entry-Ukak_siivaan" class="skt-link">Kakṣīvān</a> <a href="#entry-Ugautama" class="skt-link">Gautama</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uca_n_da_1" class="skt-link">Caṇḍa_1</a> — <a href="#entry-ca_n_dakauzika" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Caṇḍakauśika_1, drame dû à Kṣemīśvara.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_n_damahaasena">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caṇḍamahāsena</i> [<a href="#entry-mahaasena" class="skt-link">mahāsena</a>] <a href="#entry-ca_n_damahaasena" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du roi Caṇḍamahāsena d'<a href="#entry-Uavanti" class="skt-link">Avanti</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Upradyota" class="skt-link">Pradyota</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_n_dasi_mha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caṇḍasiṃha</i> [<a href="#entry-si_mha" class="skt-link">siṃha</a>] <a href="#entry-ca_n_dasi_mha" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. [DKC.] np. de Caṇḍasiṃha «Lion furieux», roi de <a href="#entry-Ukaazii" class="skt-link">Kāśī</a>; par son épouse <a href="#entry-Uliilaavatii_1" class="skt-link">Līlāvatī_1</a> il est père de <a href="#entry-Uca_n_dagho_sa" class="skt-link">Caṇḍaghoṣa</a> et <a href="#entry-Ukaantimatii" class="skt-link">Kāntimatī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-ca_n_da" class="skt-link">caṇḍa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_n_davega">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caṇḍavega</i> [<a href="#entry-vega" class="skt-link">vega</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ca_n_davega" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ca_n_davega" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ca_n_davegaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caṇḍavegā</i> au cours impétueux (se dit de la mer, du temps, d'une armée) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Caṇḍavega «Torrent furieux», chef des <a href="#entry-gandharvaas" class="skt-link">gandharvās</a> qui pillèrent le royaume de <a href="#entry-Upura_njana" class="skt-link">Purañjana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_n_dii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चण्डी</span> <i>caṇḍī</i> [f. <a href="#entry-ca_n_da" class="skt-link">caṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-ca_n_dii" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Caṇḍī ou <a href="#entry-Uca_n_dikaa" class="skt-link">Caṇḍikā</a> «la Furieuse», épith. d'un aspect terrible [<a href="#entry-mahaavidyaa" class="skt-link">mahāvidyā</a>] de la Déesse [<a href="#entry-Uzakti" class="skt-link">Śakti</a>]; elle est patronne des chasseurs tribaux [<a href="#entry-zabara" class="skt-link">śabara</a>] qui lui offrent des sacrifices humains; elle est divinité tutélaire de <a href="#entry-Uka_taka" class="skt-link">Kaṭaka</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. de la Caṇḍī_1, partie du <a href="#entry-Umaarka_n_deya" class="skt-link">Mārkaṇḍeya</a> <a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a> qui décrit les actes glorieux de la Déesse; elle est récitée not. durant la <a href="#entry-durgaapuujaa" class="skt-link">durgāpūjā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_n_diizataka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caṇḍīśataka</i> [<a href="#entry-zataka" class="skt-link">śataka</a>] <a href="#entry-ca_n_diizataka" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Caṇḍīśataka, poème à la gloire de <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a>, dû à <a href="#entry-Ubaa_na_2" class="skt-link">Bāṇa_2</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-ca_n_dii" class="skt-link">caṇḍī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_n_dika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चण्डिक</span> <i>caṇḍika</i> [<a href="#entry-ca_n_da" class="skt-link">caṇḍa</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ca_n_dika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ca_n_dika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ca_n_dikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caṇḍikā</i> circoncis — <a href="#entry-ca_n_dikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caṇḍikā</i> myth. np. de Caṇḍikā «la Furieuse», l'une des 10 puissances terribles [<a href="#entry-mahaavidyaa" class="skt-link">mahāvidyā</a>] de la Déesse [<a href="#entry-Uzakti" class="skt-link">Śakti</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-Uca_n_dii" class="skt-link">Caṇḍī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_n_diman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चण्डिमन्</span> <i>caṇḍiman</i> [<a href="#entry-ca_n_da" class="skt-link">caṇḍa</a><a href="#entry--man" class="skt-link">-man</a>] <a href="#entry-ca_n_diman" class="skt-link">m.</a> caractère colérique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चण</span> <i>caṇa</i> a. <a href="#entry-ca_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ca_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ca_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caṇā</i> ifc. réputé pour ⟨iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-ca_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> pois chiche.<br> <i>vidyācaṇa</i> réputé pour son savoir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_naka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caṇaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-ca_naka" class="skt-link">m.</a> bot. <i>Cicer arientinum</i>, pois chiche<br> <a href="#entry-ca_na" class="skt-link">caṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_saala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चषाल</span> <i>caṣāla</i> <a href="#entry-ca_saala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ca_saala" class="skt-link">n.</a> groin d'un sanglier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. véd. anneau de bois octogonal couronnant le poteau sacrificiel [<a href="#entry-yuupa" class="skt-link">yūpa</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_saka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चषक</span> <i>caṣaka</i> <a href="#entry-ca_saka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ca_saka" class="skt-link">n.</a> tasse, gobelet, verre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_t">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">चट्</span> <i>caṭ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(caṭati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>caṭita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ut</i><i>)</i> avoir lieu; se trouver; échoir à ⟨loc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_tu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चटु</span> <i>caṭu</i> <a href="#entry-ca_tu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ca_tu" class="skt-link">n.</a> gazouillis amoureux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> discours flatteur, compliments.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_tula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caṭula</i> [<a href="#entry--la" class="skt-link">-la</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ca_tula" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ca_tula" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ca_tulaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caṭulā</i> tremblant, instable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gracieux, charmant — <a href="#entry-ca_tula" class="skt-link">n.</a> pl. <i>caṭulāni</i> mots doux, flatteries.<br> <a href="#entry-ca_tu" class="skt-link">caṭu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caa_n_daala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चाण्डाल</span> <i>cāṇḍāla</i> <a href="#entry-caa_n_daala" class="skt-link">m.</a> hors caste, paria; syn. <a href="#entry-ca_n_daala" class="skt-link">caṇḍāla</a> — ifc. le pire des — <a href="#entry-caa_n_daalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cāṇḍālī</i> femme hors-caste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caa_nakya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चाणक्य</span> <i>cāṇakya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ca_naka" class="skt-link">caṇaka</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-caa_nakya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Cāṇakya, patr. de <a href="#entry-Ukau_tilya" class="skt-link">Kauṭilya</a>, ministre de <a href="#entry-Ucandragupta" class="skt-link">Candragupta</a> <a href="#entry-Umaurya" class="skt-link">Maurya</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nugupta" class="skt-link">Viṣṇugupta</a>, <a href="#entry-Umudraaraak_sasa" class="skt-link">Mudrārākṣasa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caa_nakyaniiti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cāṇakyanīti</i> [<a href="#entry-niiti" class="skt-link">nīti</a>] <a href="#entry-caa_nakyaniiti" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Cāṇakyanīti ou Cāṇakyarājanītiśāstra, ouvrage attribué à <a href="#entry-Ukau_tilya" class="skt-link">Kauṭilya</a> destiné à l'enseignement des princes, proche de la <a href="#entry-Ub_rhaspatisa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">Bṛhaspatisaṃhitā</a> du <a href="#entry-Ugaru_dapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Garuḍapurāṇa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-caa_nakya" class="skt-link">cāṇakya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caa_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चाष</span> <i>cāṣa</i> <a href="#entry-caa_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. <i>Caracias indica</i>, geai bleu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caa_tu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चाटु</span> <i>cāṭu</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ca_tu" class="skt-link">caṭu</a>] <a href="#entry-caa_tu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caa_tu" class="skt-link">n.</a> flatterie, mots d'amour; caresses.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caahuvaa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चाहुवाण</span> <i>cāhuvāṇa</i> <a href="#entry-caahuvaa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. dynastie <i>rājput</i>, se revendiquant de la lignée du Feu [<a href="#entry-agniva_mza" class="skt-link">agnivaṃśa</a>]; elle fut la dernière dynastie hindoue à régner sur Delhi et Ajmer au 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caak_su_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चाक्षुष</span> <i>cākṣuṣa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-cak_sus" class="skt-link">cakṣus</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caak_su_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caak_su_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caak_su_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cākṣuṣī</i> relatif à l'œil ou à la vision — <a href="#entry-caak_su_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Cākṣuṣa «le Visionnaire», 6⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-manu" class="skt-link">manu</a> du <a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">kalpa</a> — <a href="#entry-caak_su_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cākṣuṣī</i> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] science [<a href="#entry-vidyaa" class="skt-link">vidyā</a>] de vision extralucide; le <a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a> <a href="#entry-Uafgaarapar_na" class="skt-link">Aṅgāraparṇa</a>, qui la possédait, l'enseigna à <a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a> qui l'avait vaincu au combat.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caak_su_siividyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cākṣuṣīvidyā</i> [<a href="#entry-vidyaa" class="skt-link">vidyā</a>] <a href="#entry-caak_su_siividyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-caak_su_sii" class="skt-link">cākṣuṣī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-caak_su_sa" class="skt-link">cākṣuṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caakacakya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चाकचक्य</span> <i>cākacakya</i> <a href="#entry-caakacakya" class="skt-link">n.</a> radiance; beauté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>cākacikya</i> illusion; erreur de perception.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caal">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चाल्</span> <i>cāl</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-cal" class="skt-link">cal</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(cālayati)</i> agiter, remuer; détourner de ⟨abl.⟩, chasser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चाल</span> <i>cāla</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-cal" class="skt-link">cal</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cālā</i> qui se déplace; mobile.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caalaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चालक</span> <i>cālaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-cal" class="skt-link">cal</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caalaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caalaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caalikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cālikā</i> conducteur — <a href="#entry-caalikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cālikā</i> conductrice.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caalana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चालन</span> <i>cālana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-cal" class="skt-link">cal</a>] <a href="#entry-caalana" class="skt-link">n.</a> action de remuer, agitation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> force motrice.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caalukya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चालुक्य</span> <i>cālukya</i> <a href="#entry-caalukya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. nom de la dynastie Cālukya du Deccan; elle fut fondée au <a href="#entry-Ukar_naa_ta" class="skt-link">Karṇāṭa</a> par le prince <a href="#entry-Ujayasi_mha" class="skt-link">Jayasiṃha</a> au 5⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, à <a href="#entry-Uaaryapura" class="skt-link">Āryapura</a> (mod. Aihole), berceau de l'art des Cālukyās; en 525 le roi Rāṇāraṅga édifia le sanctuaire de Mahākuteśvara; le roi <a href="#entry-Upulakezii_1" class="skt-link">Pulakeśī_1</a> déplaça la capitale à <a href="#entry-Uvaataapi" class="skt-link">Vātāpi</a> (mod. Badāmi) en 550; <a href="#entry-Upulakezii_2" class="skt-link">Pulakeśī_2</a> étendit l'empire au Sud au détriment des <a href="#entry-Upallavaas" class="skt-link">Pallavās</a> et à l'Est; il arrêta <a href="#entry-Uhar_sa" class="skt-link">Harṣa</a> au Nord par une victoire au bord de la <a href="#entry-Unarmadaa" class="skt-link">Narmadā</a>; le roi <a href="#entry-Uvikramaaditya_1" class="skt-link">Vikramāditya_1</a> établit les chroniques de la dynastie en 658; il captura <a href="#entry-Ukaa_nciipuram" class="skt-link">Kāñcīpuram</a> en 671; le dernier <a href="#entry-Ucaalukya" class="skt-link">Cālukya</a> de Badāmi fut vaincu en 753; les <a href="#entry-Ucaalukyaas" class="skt-link">Cālukyās</a> de l'Est maintinrent un royaume de capitale <a href="#entry-Uvefgii" class="skt-link">Veṅgī</a>; plus tard (10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle) les <a href="#entry-Ucaalukyaas" class="skt-link">Cālukyās</a> de l'Ouest établirent leur capitale à <a href="#entry-Ukalyaa_nii" class="skt-link">Kalyāṇī</a>; leur roi <a href="#entry-Uvikramaaditya_6" class="skt-link">Vikramāditya_6</a> fonda en 1075 l'ère [<a href="#entry-sa_mvat" class="skt-link">saṃvat</a>] <a href="#entry-Uvikramacaalukya" class="skt-link">Vikramacālukya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caamara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चामर</span> <i>cāmara</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-camara" class="skt-link">camara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caamara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caamara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caamaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cāmarā</i> relatif au yack — <a href="#entry-caamara" class="skt-link">n.</a> chasse-mouches, panache (en queue de yack) — <a href="#entry-caamara" class="skt-link">m.</a> coquillage zoo. <i>Cypraea moneta</i>, utilisé comme monnaie — <a href="#entry-caamarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cāmarī</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hi. <i>kaurī</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caamiikara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चामीकर</span> <i>cāmīkara</i> <a href="#entry-caamiikara" class="skt-link">n.</a> or.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caamu_n_daa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चामुण्डा</span> <i>cāmuṇḍā</i> [<a href="#entry-carmamu_n_daa" class="skt-link">carmamuṇḍā</a>] <a href="#entry-caamu_n_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Cāmuṇḍā «Peau chauve», une des dix puissances terribles [<a href="#entry-mahaavidyaa" class="skt-link">mahāvidyā</a>] de la Déesse [<a href="#entry-Uzakti" class="skt-link">Śakti</a>]; noire, désséchée, vieille et hideuse, elle a la langue pendante; sa monture est un cadavre [<a href="#entry-zavaaruu_dha" class="skt-link">śavārūḍha</a>]; elle est classée comme <a href="#entry-saptamaat_rkaa" class="skt-link">saptamātṛkā</a> en tant que <a href="#entry-zakti" class="skt-link">śakti</a> de <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>cāmuṇḍī</i> myth. on l'appelle aussi Cāmuṇḍī.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caandra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चान्द्र</span> <i>cāndra</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-candra" class="skt-link">candra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caandra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caandra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caandraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cāndrā</i> lunaire; relatif à <a href="#entry-Ucandra" class="skt-link">Candra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. relatif à la grammaire de <a href="#entry-Ucandragomii" class="skt-link">Candragomī</a> — <a href="#entry-caandrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cāndrī</i> lumière lunaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caandramaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cāndramāna</i> [<a href="#entry-maana_2" class="skt-link">māna_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caandramaana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caandramaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caandramaanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cāndramānā</i> (calendrier) lunaire (opp. <a href="#entry-sauramaana" class="skt-link">sauramāna</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caandramaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cāndramāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-maasa" class="skt-link">māsa</a>] <a href="#entry-caandramaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> mois lunaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caandrasa_mvatsara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cāndrasaṃvatsara</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_mvatsara" class="skt-link">saṃvatsara</a>] <a href="#entry-caandrasa_mvatsara" class="skt-link">m.</a> année lunaire.<br> <a href="#entry-caandra" class="skt-link">cāndra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caandravatsara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cāndravatsara</i> [<a href="#entry-vatsara" class="skt-link">vatsara</a>] <a href="#entry-caandravatsara" class="skt-link">m.</a> année lunaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caandravyaakara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cāndravyākaraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-vyaakara_na" class="skt-link">vyākaraṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-caandravyaakara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. np. du Cāndravyākaraṇa «Grammaire de <a href="#entry-Ucandragomii" class="skt-link">Candragomī</a>», du 6⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; c'est une grammaire sanskrite destinée aux bouddhistes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caandrayoga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cāndrayoga</i> [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] <a href="#entry-caandrayoga" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. cf. <a href="#entry-yogaas" class="skt-link">yogās</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चाप</span> <i>cāpa</i> <a href="#entry-caapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caapa" class="skt-link">n.</a> arc; arc-en-ciel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de la dynastie Cāpa; cf. <a href="#entry-Ua_nahilapaa_taka" class="skt-link">Aṇahilapāṭaka</a>.<br> <i>cāpapāṇi</i> m. qui tient l'arc en main (se dit not. de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caapadhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cāpadhara</i> [<a href="#entry-dhara" class="skt-link">dhara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caapadhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caapadhara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caapadharaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cāpadharā</i> qui tient un arc.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caapagu_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cāpaguṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-caapagu_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> corde d'arc.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caapala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चापल</span> <i>cāpala</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-capala" class="skt-link">capala</a>] <a href="#entry-caapala" class="skt-link">n.</a> légèreté, agilité, vivacité; frivolité, insouciance, étourderie; enfantillage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caapapuujaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cāpapūjā</i> [<a href="#entry-puujaa" class="skt-link">pūjā</a>] <a href="#entry-caapapuujaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. du festival Cāpapūjā d'archerie auquel <a href="#entry-Uka_msa" class="skt-link">Kaṃsa</a> invita <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> et <a href="#entry-Ubalaraama" class="skt-link">Balarāma</a> afin de les tuer; cf. <a href="#entry-Uakruura" class="skt-link">Akrūra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-caapa" class="skt-link">cāpa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caapin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चापिन्</span> <i>cāpin</i> [<a href="#entry-caapa" class="skt-link">cāpa</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] <a href="#entry-caapin" class="skt-link">m.</a> archer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चार</span> <i>cāra</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-car" class="skt-link">car</a>] <a href="#entry-caara" class="skt-link">m.</a> espion — <a href="#entry-caara" class="skt-link">m.</a> mouvement, course; voyage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pratique, exercice.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चारण</span> <i>cāraṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-car" class="skt-link">car</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caara_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caara_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cāraṇā</i> véd. qui concerne une école védique — <a href="#entry-caara_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> baladin, chanteur ambulant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mod. caste du Gujarāt et du Rajasthan; ses membres sont respectés pour leur courage et leur loyauté; ils sont prisés comme otages; trahis, ils pratiquent l'auto-immolation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lime.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caaraaya_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cārāyaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-aayana" class="skt-link">āyana</a>] <a href="#entry-caaraaya_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. patr. de Cārāyaṇa, grammairien antique mentionné par <a href="#entry-Upata_njali" class="skt-link">Patañjali</a>; il est aussi le promoteur [<a href="#entry-pravakt_r" class="skt-link">pravaktṛ</a>] de l'école éponyme [<a href="#entry-caaraaya_niiya" class="skt-link">cārāyaṇīya</a>] du <a href="#entry-Uyajurveda" class="skt-link">Yajurveda</a> noir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caaraaya_niiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cārāyaṇīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caaraaya_niiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caaraaya_niiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caaraaya_niiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cārāyaṇīyā</i> relatif à <a href="#entry-Ucaaraaya_na" class="skt-link">Cārāyaṇa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. école du <a href="#entry-Uyajurveda" class="skt-link">Yajurveda</a> noir.<br> <a href="#entry-caaraaya_na" class="skt-link">cārāyaṇa</a> <br> <a href="#entry-caara" class="skt-link">cāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caaraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चारक</span> <i>cāraka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-car" class="skt-link">car</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caaraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caaraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caarikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cārikā</i> qui met en mouvement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. composé par <a href="#entry-Ucaraka" class="skt-link">Caraka</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> associé, compagnon — <a href="#entry-caarika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <i>cārika</i> ifc. qui pratique ⟨iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-caarikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cārikā</i> dame de compagnie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caarakiina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चारकीन</span> <i>cārakīna</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-caraka" class="skt-link">caraka</a><a href="#entry--iina" class="skt-link">-īna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caarakiina" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caarakiina" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caarakiinaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cārakīnā</i> bon pour les étudiants nomades.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caarin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चारिन्</span> <i>cārin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-car" class="skt-link">car</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caarin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cāriṇī</i> qui fait, qui agit, qui pratique; qui se déplace, qui circule — <a href="#entry-caarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> fantassin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caaritra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चारित्र</span> <i>cāritra</i> [instr. <a href="#entry-car" class="skt-link">car</a>] <a href="#entry-caaritra" class="skt-link">n.</a> conduite, comportement, attitude <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bonne conduite, attitude correcte; bonne réputation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> observance; coutume.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caaru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चारु</span> <i>cāru</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-canas" class="skt-link">canas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caaru" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caaru" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caarvii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cārvī</i> serein, doux, aimable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> beau, agréable, plaisant, charmant, gracieux, joli; estimé, cher — <a href="#entry-caaru" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Cāru «Charmant», fils de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Ub_rhaspati" class="skt-link">Bṛhaspati</a> — <a href="#entry-caarvii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cārvī</i> belle femme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Cārvī «Charmante», épouse de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>carus</i>; fr. cher.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caarucaryaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cārucaryā</i> [<a href="#entry-caryaa" class="skt-link">caryā</a>] <a href="#entry-caarucaryaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Cārucaryā «Bonne conduite», traité d'hygiène et de morale en 100 vers attribué au roi <a href="#entry-Ubhojadeva" class="skt-link">Bhojadeva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caarudarzana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cārudarśana</i> [<a href="#entry-darzana" class="skt-link">darśana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caarudarzana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caarudarzana" class="skt-link">n.</a> de plaisante apparence.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caarudatta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cārudatta</i> [<a href="#entry-datta" class="skt-link">datta</a>] <a href="#entry-caarudatta" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. de Cārudatta «Doué de grâce», brâhmane ruiné, principal personnage de la <a href="#entry-Um_rcchaka_tikaa" class="skt-link">Mṛcchakaṭikā</a>; le <a href="#entry-viduu_saka" class="skt-link">vidūṣaka</a> Maitreya_1 est son ami, la courtisane <a href="#entry-Uvasantasenaa" class="skt-link">Vasantasenā</a> en est éprise; l'enfant <a href="#entry-Urohasena" class="skt-link">Rohasena</a> est son fils de son épouse <a href="#entry-Udhuutaa" class="skt-link">Dhūtā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. de Cārudatta_1, pièce dramatique de <a href="#entry-Ubhaasa" class="skt-link">Bhāsa</a>, texte précurseur de la <a href="#entry-Um_rcchaka_tikaa" class="skt-link">Mṛcchakaṭikā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caarutaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cārutā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-caarutaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> beauté, grâce; charme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caarutama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cārutama</i> [<a href="#entry--tama" class="skt-link">-tama</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caarutama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caarutama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caarutamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cārutamā</i> super. le plus charmant, le plus beau; le mieux aimé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caarutara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cārutara</i> [<a href="#entry--tara" class="skt-link">-tara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caarutara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caarutara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caarutaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cārutarā</i> compar. plus charmant; très gracieux, très aimé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caarutva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cārutva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-caarutva" class="skt-link">n.</a> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caarvaac">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cārvāc</i> [<a href="#entry-vaac" class="skt-link">vāc</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caarvaac" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caarvaac" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caarvaac" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui parle aimablement.<br> <a href="#entry-caaru" class="skt-link">cāru</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caarvaaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चार्वाक</span> <i>cārvāka</i> [<a href="#entry-caarvaac" class="skt-link">cārvāc</a>] <a href="#entry-caarvaaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du vampire [<a href="#entry-raak_sasa" class="skt-link">rākṣasa</a>] Cārvāka_1 «au Verbe agréable», ami de <a href="#entry-Uduryodhana" class="skt-link">Duryodhana</a>; il se déguisa en brâhmane mendiant lors du retour triomphal de <a href="#entry-Uyudhi_s_thira" class="skt-link">Yudhiṣṭhira</a> à <a href="#entry-Uhastinaapura" class="skt-link">Hastināpura</a> pour l'injurier, mais fut reconnu, brûlé vif et réduit en cendres par le regard des brâhmanes <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Cārvāka, philosophe sceptique probablement mythique, professant le matérialisme [<a href="#entry-lokaayata" class="skt-link">lokāyata</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>cārvākās</i> les matérialistes, adeptes de <a href="#entry-Ucaarvaaka" class="skt-link">Cārvāka</a>; ils sont athées [<a href="#entry-naastika" class="skt-link">nāstika</a>] et ne croient qu'aux témoignages de leurs sens; ils n'admettent comme moyen de connaissance légitime [<a href="#entry-pramaa_na" class="skt-link">pramāṇa</a>] que la constatation directe par perception [<a href="#entry-pratyak_sa" class="skt-link">pratyakṣa</a>]; ils sont hédonistes et cherchent à mener une vie agréable, symbolisée par le beurre [<a href="#entry-gh_rta" class="skt-link">ghṛta</a>]; ils rejettent la transmigration des âmes et l'au-delà, l'autorité du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> et les distinctions de caste; ils érigent la liberté de pensée en principe fondamental; cf. <a href="#entry-Ub_rhaspati_1" class="skt-link">Bṛhaspati_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चार्य</span> <i>cārya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-car" class="skt-link">car</a>] <a href="#entry-caarya" class="skt-link">n.</a> espionnage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चात्</span> <i>cāt</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-cat" class="skt-link">cat</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(cātayati)</i> faire partir, chasser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caataka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चातक</span> <i>cātaka</i> <a href="#entry-caataka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caatakii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cātakī</i> natu. zoo. <i>Cucculus melanoleucus</i> ou zoo. <i>Clamator jacobinus</i>, coucou jacobin, oiseau noir et blanc; toujours assoiffé, on dit qu'il se nourrit des gouttes de pluie; syn. <a href="#entry-saarafga" class="skt-link">sāraṅga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caatana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चातन</span> <i>cātana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-caat" class="skt-link">cāt</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caatana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caatana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caatanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cātanā</i> qui chasse, qui supprime.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caatura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चातुर</span> <i>cātura</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-catura" class="skt-link">catura</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caatura" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caatura" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caaturii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cāturī</i> intelligent, ingénieux — <a href="#entry-caaturii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cāturī</i> intelligence, habileté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caaturmaasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चातुर्मास्य</span> <i>cāturmāsya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-caturmaasa" class="skt-link">caturmāsa</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-caaturmaasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. début d'une période de 4 mois; sacrifice fait à cette occasion; ces trois rituels saisonniers se nomment <a href="#entry-vaizvadeva" class="skt-link">vaiśvadeva</a>, <a href="#entry-varu_napraghaasaas" class="skt-link">varuṇapraghāsās</a> et <a href="#entry-saakamedhaas" class="skt-link">sākamedhās</a> — a. <a href="#entry-caaturmaasya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caaturmaasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caaturmaasyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cāturmāsyā</i> relatif à ces sacrifices.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caaturvaidya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चातुर्वैद्य</span> <i>cāturvaidya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-caturvidya" class="skt-link">caturvidya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caaturvaidya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caaturvaidya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caaturvaidyii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cāturvaidyī</i> soc. qui maîtrise les 4 Védas; syn. <a href="#entry-caturvedin" class="skt-link">caturvedin</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caaturvar_nya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चातुर्वर्ण्य</span> <i>cāturvarṇya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-caturvar_na" class="skt-link">caturvarṇa</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-caaturvar_nya" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. ensemble des quatre classes védiques.<br> <i>cāturvarṇyaṃ</i> <i>mayā</i> <i>sṛṣṭaṃ</i> <i>guṇakarmavibhāgaśaḥ</i> [BhG.] J'ai créé les quatre classes en rétribution des mérites et des actes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caaturya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चातुर्य</span> <i>cāturya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-catura" class="skt-link">catura</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-caaturya" class="skt-link">n.</a> habileté; charme, amabilité; coquetterie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cafkram">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चङ्क्रम्</span> <i>caṅkram</i> [int. <a href="#entry-kram" class="skt-link">kram</a>] v. pr. <i>(caṅkramīti)</i> toujours dominer — v. pr. md. <i>(caṅkramyate)</i> flâner, se promener.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cafkrama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चङ्क्रम</span> <i>caṅkrama</i> [act. <a href="#entry-cafkram" class="skt-link">caṅkram</a>] <a href="#entry-cafkrama" class="skt-link">m.</a> endroit de promenade — <a href="#entry-cafkramaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caṅkramā</i> promenade <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. première promenade du <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> après son Éveil.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caitanya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चैतन्य</span> <i>caitanya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-cetana" class="skt-link">cetana</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-caitanya" class="skt-link">n.</a> esprit, conscience, sensation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] conscience éternelle, spiritualité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vaize_sika" class="skt-link">vaiśeṣika</a>] conscience du monde sensible — <a href="#entry-caitanya" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. titre de novice [<a href="#entry-brahmacaarin" class="skt-link">brahmacārin</a>] de l'ordre <a href="#entry-Ubhaaratii" class="skt-link">Bhāratī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. np. de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> Caitanya «Conscience de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>» (1486–1533), mystique <a href="#entry-vai_s_nava" class="skt-link">vaiṣṇava</a>, né <a href="#entry-Uvizvambhara" class="skt-link">Viśvambhara</a> <a href="#entry-Umizra" class="skt-link">Miśra</a> fils de <a href="#entry-Ujagannaatha_2" class="skt-link">Jagannātha_2</a> à <a href="#entry-Umaayaapura" class="skt-link">Māyāpura</a> près de <a href="#entry-Unavadviipa" class="skt-link">Navadvīpa</a> au nord du Gange au Bengale; sa mère Sacī Devī l'appelait <a href="#entry-Uniimaaya" class="skt-link">Nīmāya</a>; il fut un expert de sanskrit et de rhétorique [<a href="#entry-pa_n_dita" class="skt-link">paṇḍita</a>] très jeune, et forma de nombreux élèves; il se maria à l'âge de 15 ans; à la suite d'une expérience extatique en 1509, il fut initié par <a href="#entry-Ukezava_1" class="skt-link">Keśava_1</a>, moine de l'ordre <a href="#entry-Ubhaaratii" class="skt-link">Bhāratī</a> de <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a>, mais suivit une doctrine proche de celle de <a href="#entry-Uraamaanuja" class="skt-link">Rāmānuja</a>; il prêcha la dévotion [<a href="#entry-bhakti" class="skt-link">bhakti</a>] à <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> par le chant dévotionnel [<a href="#entry-kiirtana" class="skt-link">kīrtana</a>], la récitation du <a href="#entry-mahaamantra" class="skt-link">mahāmantra</a> et la danse extatique; il était d'une beauté éclatante [<a href="#entry-Ugauraafga" class="skt-link">Gaurāṅga</a>]; il se retira comme <a href="#entry-sannyaasin" class="skt-link">sannyāsin</a> à <a href="#entry-Upurii" class="skt-link">Purī</a> en 1515; il représente <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> réincarné comme Son propre disciple, et il est vénéré comme <a href="#entry-Umahaaprabhu" class="skt-link">Mahāprabhu</a>; on le représente dans une pose de danse extatique, les bras levés au ciel; <a href="#entry-Uvizvaruupa_2" class="skt-link">Viśvarūpa_2</a> était son frère aîné; <a href="#entry-Unityaananda" class="skt-link">Nityānanda</a> fut son ami et disciple; cf. <a href="#entry-Ugauracandra" class="skt-link">Gauracandra</a>, <a href="#entry-Uprabhupaada" class="skt-link">Prabhupāda</a>, <a href="#entry-Uzik_saa_s_taka" class="skt-link">Śikṣāṣṭaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caitanyabhaagavata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caitanyabhāgavata</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaagavata" class="skt-link">bhāgavata</a>] <a href="#entry-caitanyabhaagavata" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Caitanyabhāgavata, biographie de <a href="#entry-Ucaitanya" class="skt-link">Caitanya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caitanyasvaruupa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caitanyasvarūpa</i> [<a href="#entry-svaruupa" class="skt-link">svarūpa</a>] <a href="#entry-caitanyasvaruupa" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. état conscient.<br> <a href="#entry-caitanya" class="skt-link">caitanya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caitra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चैत्र</span> <i>caitra</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-citraa" class="skt-link">citrā</a>] <a href="#entry-caitra" class="skt-link">m.</a> mois de <a href="#entry-Ucitraa" class="skt-link">Citrā</a> (mars-avril); il débute l'année lunaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. moine errant bd. ou jaïn <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> nom d'homme commun; «Untel» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. patr. Caitra «Fils de <a href="#entry-Ucitraa" class="skt-link">Citrā</a>»; np. d'un fils de <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> — <a href="#entry-caitrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caitrī</i> soc. sa pleine lune; on y pratique un <a href="#entry-paakayaj_na" class="skt-link">pākayajña</a>; c'est le jour d'anniversaire [<a href="#entry-jayantii_2" class="skt-link">jayantī_2</a>] de <a href="#entry-Uhanumaan" class="skt-link">Hanumān</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caitraratha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चैत्ररथ</span> <i>caitraratha</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-citraratha" class="skt-link">citraratha</a>] <a href="#entry-caitraratha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Caitraratha «fruit du travail de <a href="#entry-Ucitraratha" class="skt-link">Citraratha</a>», merveilleux jardin de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a> sur le Mont <a href="#entry-Umandara" class="skt-link">Mandara</a>; son jardinier en est <a href="#entry-Ucitraratha" class="skt-link">Citraratha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caitya_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चैत्य</span> <i>caitya_1</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-cit_2" class="skt-link">cit_2</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-caitya_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> âme individuelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caitya_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चैत्य</span> <i>caitya_2</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-citaa" class="skt-link">citā</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-caitya_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caitya_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> monument funéraire; chapelle annexe d'un temple <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. objet de culte; arbre sacré; pierre sacrée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. sanctuaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jn. représentation [<a href="#entry-muurti" class="skt-link">mūrti</a>] d'un saint [<a href="#entry-jina" class="skt-link">jina</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caityaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caityaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-caityaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Caityaka «Sanctuaire», l'une des 5 collines entourant la ville de <a href="#entry-Ugirivraja" class="skt-link">Girivraja</a>; elle portait un sanctuaire où l'on frappait trois tambours faits avec la peau de <a href="#entry-raak_sasaas" class="skt-link">rākṣasās</a>; c'est par là qu'arrivèrent <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a> et <a href="#entry-Ubhiima" class="skt-link">Bhīma</a> lorsqu'ils attaquèrent <a href="#entry-Ujaraasandha" class="skt-link">Jarāsandha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caityavandana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caityavandana</i> [<a href="#entry-vandana" class="skt-link">vandana</a>] <a href="#entry-caityavandana" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. vénération des icônes et des temples.<br> <a href="#entry-caitya_2" class="skt-link">caitya_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cak_rvas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चकृवस्</span> <i>cakṛvas</i> [ppft. <a href="#entry-k_r_1" class="skt-link">kṛ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-cak_rvas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-cak_rvas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-cakru_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cakruṣī</i> ayant fait — <a href="#entry-cak_rvas" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. parfait (d'un verbe quelconque).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cak_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चक्ष्</span> <i>cakṣ</i> [red. <a href="#entry-kaaz" class="skt-link">kāś</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. md. <i>(caṣṭe)</i> pr. md. <i>(cakṣate)</i> imp. <i>(cakṣva)</i> inf. <i>(cakṣṭum)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>caṣṭa</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ā</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> voir, apercevoir, regarder <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> annoncer; dire, parler de ⟨acc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cak_su_h">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चक्षुः</span> <i>cakṣuḥ</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-cak_sus" class="skt-link">cakṣus</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cak_su_hzrotra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cakṣuḥśrotra</i> [<a href="#entry-zrotra" class="skt-link">śrotra</a>] <a href="#entry-cak_su_hzrotra" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. la vision et l'ouïe.<br> <a href="#entry-cak_su_h" class="skt-link">cakṣuḥ</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cak_su_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चक्षुष्</span> <i>cakṣuṣ</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-cak_sus" class="skt-link">cakṣus</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cak_su_ska">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चक्षुष्क</span> <i>cakṣuṣka</i> [<a href="#entry-cak_sus" class="skt-link">cakṣus</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-cak_su_ska" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-cak_su_ska" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-cak_su_skaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cakṣuṣkā</i> qui n'a d'yeux que pour ⟨iic.⟩; qui prend le point de vue de ⟨iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cak_sus">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चक्षुस्</span> <i>cakṣus</i> [<a href="#entry-cak_s" class="skt-link">cakṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-cak_sus" class="skt-link">n.</a> œil; vue, vision <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] le sens [<a href="#entry-buddhiindriya" class="skt-link">buddhīndriya</a>] de la vue; son régent [<a href="#entry-niyant_r" class="skt-link">niyantṛ</a>] est <a href="#entry-Uaaditya" class="skt-link">Āditya</a>; la perception [<a href="#entry-tanmaatra" class="skt-link">tanmātra</a>] associéee est la forme [<a href="#entry-ruupa" class="skt-link">rūpa</a>]; syn. <a href="#entry-ak_si" class="skt-link">akṣi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cak_suu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चक्षू</span> <i>cakṣū</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-cak_sus" class="skt-link">cakṣus</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cak_suuraaga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cakṣūrāga</i> [<a href="#entry-raaga" class="skt-link">rāga</a>] <a href="#entry-cak_suuraaga" class="skt-link">m.</a> coup de foudre.<br> <a href="#entry-cak_suu" class="skt-link">cakṣū</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चकार</span> <i>cakāra</i> [<i>(ca)</i>-<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-cakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> le son ou la lettre ‘<i>ca</i>’.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakaas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">चकास्</span> <i>cakās</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-kaaz" class="skt-link">kāś</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(cakāsti)</i> pft. péri. <i>(cakāsām)</i><i>(cakāra)</i> briller.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चकित</span> <i>cakita</i> a. <a href="#entry-cakita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-cakita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-cakitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cakitā</i> tremblant, craintif, effrayé, intimidé — <a href="#entry-cakita" class="skt-link">n.</a> inquiétude; alarme.<br> <i>cakitam</i> en tremblant, avec crainte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakora">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चकोर</span> <i>cakora</i> <a href="#entry-cakora" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. <i>Perdix rufa</i>, perdrix rouge; on dit qu'elle se nourrit des rayons lunaires, et que ses yeux deviennent rouges devant la nourriture empoisonnée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चक्र</span> <i>cakra</i> <a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">n.</a> roue, cercle, disque; cycle, mouvement circulaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> la roue royale, insigne de souveraineté; armée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> région, pays; souveraineté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. disque flamboyant, arme solaire de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Usudarzana" class="skt-link">Sudarśana</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [tantr.] diagramme mystique circulaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] centre d'énergie psychique, dont les 7 principaux sont: <a href="#entry-muulaadhaara" class="skt-link">mūlādhāra</a>, <a href="#entry-svaadhi_s_thaana" class="skt-link">svādhiṣṭhāna</a>, <a href="#entry-ma_nipuura" class="skt-link">maṇipūra</a>, <a href="#entry-anaahata" class="skt-link">anāhata</a>, <a href="#entry-vizuddha" class="skt-link">viśuddha</a>, <a href="#entry-aaj_naa_2" class="skt-link">ājñā_2</a> et <a href="#entry-sahasraara" class="skt-link">sahasrāra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>circus</i>; fr. cercle; pali <i>cakka</i>.<br> <i>cakram</i> <i>car</i> voyager en chariot.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakraafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cakrāṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-afga_1" class="skt-link">aṅga_1</a>] <a href="#entry-cakraafga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-cakraafgii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cakrāṅgī</i> [«au cou courbé»] oie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. zoo. <i>Tadorna ferruginea</i>, canard rouge; syn. <a href="#entry-cakravaaka" class="skt-link">cakravāka</a>, <a href="#entry-rathaafga" class="skt-link">rathāṅga</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakraak_si">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cakrākṣi</i> [<a href="#entry-ak_si" class="skt-link">akṣi</a>] <a href="#entry-cakraak_si" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Cakrākṣi, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ujagannaatha" class="skt-link">Jagannātha</a> «aux yeux ronds»; sa décoration représente la face de Dieu contemplant les péchés du monde au <a href="#entry-kaliyuga" class="skt-link">kaliyuga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चक्रक</span> <i>cakraka</i> [<a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-cakraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-cakraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-cakrikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cakrikā</i> circulaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakrakrii_daa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cakrakrīḍā</i> [<a href="#entry-krii_daa" class="skt-link">krīḍā</a>] <a href="#entry-cakrakrii_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. [«réunion circulaire»] rituel <a href="#entry-krama" class="skt-link">krama</a> secret d'orgie collective; syn. <a href="#entry-cakramelaka" class="skt-link">cakramelaka</a>, <a href="#entry-viiramelaapa" class="skt-link">vīramelāpa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakramelaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cakramelaka</i> [<a href="#entry-melaka" class="skt-link">melaka</a>] <a href="#entry-cakramelaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. [«réunion circulaire»] rituel <a href="#entry-krama" class="skt-link">krama</a> secret d'orgie collective; syn. <a href="#entry-cakrakrii_daa" class="skt-link">cakrakrīḍā</a>, <a href="#entry-viiramelaapa" class="skt-link">vīramelāpa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakramukha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cakramukha</i> [<a href="#entry-paada" class="skt-link">pāda</a>] <a href="#entry-cakramukha" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«museau circulaire»] sanglier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakrapaa_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cakrapāṇi</i> [<a href="#entry-paa_ni" class="skt-link">pāṇi</a>] <a href="#entry-cakrapaa_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Cakrapāṇi, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> «qui tient le disque en main».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakrapaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cakrapāda</i> [<a href="#entry-paada" class="skt-link">pāda</a>] <a href="#entry-cakrapaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«pied circulaire»] éléphant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakrappa_nidatta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cakrappaṇidatta</i> [<a href="#entry-datta" class="skt-link">datta</a>] <a href="#entry-cakrappa_nidatta" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Cakrapāṇidatta, médecin bengali du 11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, principal commentateur de <a href="#entry-Ucaraka" class="skt-link">Caraka</a>, auteur du manuel ayurvédique <a href="#entry-Ucikitsaasafgraha" class="skt-link">Cikitsāsaṃgraha</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-cakrapaa_ni" class="skt-link">cakrapāṇi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakrapuru_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cakrapuruṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-puru_sa" class="skt-link">puruṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-cakrapuru_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Cakrapuruṣa, personnification de <a href="#entry-Usudarzana" class="skt-link">Sudarśana</a>, disque flamboyant de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>; on le représente associé à <a href="#entry-Ugadaadevii" class="skt-link">Gadādevī</a>-<a href="#entry-Ukaumodakii" class="skt-link">Kaumodakī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakrapuujaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cakrapūjā</i> [<a href="#entry-puujaa" class="skt-link">pūjā</a>] <a href="#entry-cakrapuujaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. [tantr.] rite d'union symbolique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakrav_rddhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cakravṛddhi</i> [<a href="#entry-v_rddhi" class="skt-link">vṛddhi</a>] <a href="#entry-cakrav_rddhi" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. intérêt composé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakravaaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cakravāka</i> [<a href="#entry-vaaka" class="skt-link">vāka</a>] <a href="#entry-cakravaaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. <i>Tadorna ferruginea</i>, sorte de canard rouge; dans la tradition poétique c'est un modèle de constance, mais les couples doivent se séparer la nuit, à cause de la malédiction d'un sage; la poitrine des jolies femmes est comparée à un couple de ces oiseaux; syn. <a href="#entry-cakraafga" class="skt-link">cakrāṅga</a>, <a href="#entry-rathaafga" class="skt-link">rathāṅga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakravala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cakravala</i> [<a href="#entry--vala" class="skt-link">-vala</a>] a. <a href="#entry-cakravala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-cakravala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-cakravalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cakravalā</i> circulaire, cyclique — <a href="#entry-cakravala" class="skt-link">m.</a> math. méthode itérative.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakravartin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cakravartin</i> [<a href="#entry-vartin" class="skt-link">vartin</a>] <a href="#entry-cakravartin" class="skt-link">m.</a> empereur, maître de l'univers; souverain [«qui fait tourner la roue de la souveraineté [<a href="#entry-ratnacakra" class="skt-link">ratnacakra</a>]»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> roi de la lignée des douze rois commençant à <a href="#entry-Ubharata" class="skt-link">Bharata</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. le <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> en majesté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pali <i>cakkavatti</i>.<br> <i>ubhayacakravartin</i> m. souverain des deux mondes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakravat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चक्रवत्</span> <i>cakravat</i> [<a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a><a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-cakravat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-cakravat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-cakravatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cakravatī</i> muni de roues <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> circulaire, pareil à une roue — <a href="#entry-cakravat" class="skt-link">m.</a> moulin à huile <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> souverain, empereur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. par ext. <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> — adv. comme une roue; en rotation.<br> <i>cakravat</i> <i>parivartante</i> <i>sukhāni</i> <i>ca</i> <i>duḥkhāni</i> <i>ca</i> Les bonheurs et les malheurs se succèdent comme la roue qui tourne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakravyuuha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cakravyūha</i> [<a href="#entry-vyuuha" class="skt-link">vyūha</a>] <a href="#entry-cakravyuuha" class="skt-link">m.</a> ordre de bataille circulaire; [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] <a href="#entry-Udro_na" class="skt-link">Droṇa</a> l'utilisa pour l'armée des <a href="#entry-Ukauravaas" class="skt-link">Kauravās</a>; <a href="#entry-Uabhimanyu" class="skt-link">Abhimanyu</a> y fut trappé traîtreusement, provoquant sa mort.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चक्रि</span> <i>cakri</i> [red. <a href="#entry-k_r_1" class="skt-link">kṛ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-cakri" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-cakri" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-cakri" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui fait; qui effectue ⟨acc.⟩; actif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakriivat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चक्रीवत्</span> <i>cakrīvat</i> [<a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a><a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-cakriivat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-cakriivat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-cakriivatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cakrīvatī</i> muni de roues <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> allant en chariot — <a href="#entry-cakriivat" class="skt-link">n.</a> chariot.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cakrin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चक्रिन्</span> <i>cakrin</i> [<a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-cakrin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-cakrin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-cakri_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cakriṇī</i> qui a des roues; qui conduit un chariot <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui porte le disque; np. de Cakrī, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> — <a href="#entry-cakrin" class="skt-link">m.</a> potier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> souverain; gouverneur de province <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> équilibriste; tricheur; informateur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cal">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">चल्</span> <i>cal</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-car" class="skt-link">car</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(calati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>calita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>calitavya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pra</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> bouger, s'agiter, être agité; partir, dévier; trembler, palpiter — ca. <i>(cālayati)</i> agiter, secouer, remuer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> détourner de, expulser de ⟨abl.⟩, chasser — int. md. <i>(cañcalyate)</i> être instable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चल</span> <i>cala</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-cal" class="skt-link">cal</a>] a. <a href="#entry-cala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-cala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-calaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>calā</i> mobile, mouvant; fragile, instable; tremblant, confus; inconstant.<br> <i>calīkṛ</i> faire bouger.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-calaacala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>calācala</i> [<a href="#entry-acala" class="skt-link">acala</a>] a. <a href="#entry-calaacala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-calaacala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-calaacalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>calācalā</i> instable, changeant, se mouvant sans cesse — <a href="#entry-calaacala" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Calācala, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «Perpétuellement changeant» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [«mobile et immobile»] les plantes et les animaux.<br> <a href="#entry-cala" class="skt-link">cala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-calana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चलन</span> <i>calana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-cal" class="skt-link">cal</a>] a. <a href="#entry-calana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-calana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-calanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>calanā</i> mobile, mouvant — <a href="#entry-calana" class="skt-link">n.</a> motion, mise en mouvement; action de marcher <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vibration.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-calat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चलत्</span> <i>calat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-cal" class="skt-link">cal</a>] a. <a href="#entry-calat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-calat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-calantii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>calantī</i> qui bouge, mobile.<br> <i>calantī</i> <i>pratimā</i> soc. image mobile de temple, utilisée pour les processions; on l'appelle <a href="#entry-utsavamuurti" class="skt-link">utsavamūrti</a> dans le Sud de l'Inde.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-calita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चलित</span> <i>calita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-cal" class="skt-link">cal</a>] a. <a href="#entry-calita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-calita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-calitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>calitā</i> tremblant, mouvant; agité, instable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> perturbé, ébranlé, dévié; détourné de ⟨abl.⟩ — <a href="#entry-calita" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de bouger, de remuer; déplacement; danse.<br> <i>prathamacalitadakṣiṇacaranaḥ</i> qui se déplace en partant du pied droit (rituel de bon augure).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-calitavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चलितव्य</span> <i>calitavya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <a href="#entry-cal" class="skt-link">cal</a>] a. <a href="#entry-calitavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-calitavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-calitavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>calitavyā</i> qui doit partir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (impers.) il faut partir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">चम्</span> <i>cam</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(camati)</i> pft. <i>(cacāma)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>camya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ā</i><i>)</i> boire; lamper <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. se rincer la bouche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-camana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चमन</span> <i>camana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-cam" class="skt-link">cam</a>] <a href="#entry-camana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de boire une gorgée
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-camara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चमर</span> <i>camara</i> <a href="#entry-camara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-camarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>camarī</i> natu. zoo. <i>Bos grunniens</i>, yack blanc du Tibet <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. sa queue, dont on fait des chasse-mouches <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ang. chowrie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-camat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चमत्</span> <i>camat</i> ind. excl. de surprise.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-camatkaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>camatkāra</i> [<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-camatkaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> étonnement, surprise; émerveillement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. not. plaisir causé par une composition littéraire; c'est l'essence du <a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>.<br> <i>yatpratipāditārthaviṣayakabhāvanātvaṃ</i> <i>camatkārajanakatāvacchedakatvaṃ</i> <i>tattvaṃ</i> <i>kāvyatvam</i> [<a href="#entry-Urasagafgaadhara" class="skt-link">Rasagaṅgādhara</a>] Seuls les mots suscitant l'émerveillement appartiennent à la poésie.<br> <a href="#entry-camat" class="skt-link">camat</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-campa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चम्प</span> <i>campa</i> <a href="#entry-campa" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Bauhinia variegata</i>, arbuste de la famille des fabacées aux grandes fleurs rose vif ou blanches au parfum suave — <a href="#entry-campaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>campā</i> géo. np. de la ville de Campā, capitale du pays <a href="#entry-Uafga" class="skt-link">Aṅga</a> (mod. Bhāgalpur au <a href="#entry-Ubihaara" class="skt-link">Bihāra</a>); c'était une ville de commerce importante <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] ville capitale de <a href="#entry-Ulomapaada" class="skt-link">Lomapāda</a> et de <a href="#entry-Ukar_na" class="skt-link">Karṇa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. du royaume hindouïsé du Campā_1 au Sud-Vietnam; il eut son apogée du 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ au 11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il comprenait les principautés (du Nord au Sud) d'<a href="#entry-Uindrapura" class="skt-link">Indrapura</a>, d'<a href="#entry-Uamaraavatii_2" class="skt-link">Amarāvatī_2</a>, de <a href="#entry-Uvijaya_5" class="skt-link">Vijaya_5</a>, de <a href="#entry-Ukau_thaara" class="skt-link">Kauṭhāra</a> et de <a href="#entry-Upaa_n_durafga_1" class="skt-link">Pāṇḍuraṅga_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-campaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>campaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-campaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Michelia champaka</i>, arbuste de la famille du magnolia; ses fleurs jaune vif sont suaves et consacrées à <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Campaka, ministre du roi <a href="#entry-Uhar_sa_1" class="skt-link">Harṣa_1</a> (11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); le poète <a href="#entry-Ukalha_na" class="skt-link">Kalhaṇa</a> est son fils.<br> <a href="#entry-campa" class="skt-link">campa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-campuu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चम्पू</span> <i>campū</i> <a href="#entry-campuu" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-kaavya" class="skt-link">kāvya</a>] style de composition littéraire élaborée dans lequel un même thème est traité successivement en prose [<a href="#entry-gadya" class="skt-link">gadya</a>] et en vers [<a href="#entry-padya" class="skt-link">padya</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_nacampuu" class="skt-link">Rāmāyaṇacampū</a>, <a href="#entry-Uyazastilakacampuu" class="skt-link">Yaśastilakacampū</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-camuu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चमू</span> <i>camū</i> <a href="#entry-camuu" class="skt-link">f.</a> armée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चन</span> <i>cana</i> part. donne une valeur indéfinie aux pr. et aux a. interr.<br> <i>kaścana</i> quelqu'un, quiconque.<br> <i>kvacana</i> quelque part; n'importe où.<br> <i>na</i> <i>kaścana</i> personne.<br> <i>kiṃcana</i> quelque chose <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>kānicana</i> quelques choses.<br> <i>na</i> <i>kiṃcana</i> rien.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-canas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चनस्</span> <i>canas</i> <a href="#entry-canas" class="skt-link">n.</a> plaisir, satisfaction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cand">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">चन्द्</span> <i>cand</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(candati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>candita</i><i>)</i> briller, resplendir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>candeo</i>; ang. candle; fr. chandelle, candide, incendie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-canda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चन्द</span> <i>canda</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-cand" class="skt-link">cand</a>] <a href="#entry-canda" class="skt-link">m.</a> Lune.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चन्दल</span> <i>candala</i> [<a href="#entry-canda" class="skt-link">canda</a><a href="#entry--la" class="skt-link">-la</a>] <a href="#entry-candala" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de la dynastie Candala ou Candela, fondée par Nannuk, chef d'un clan <i>rājput</i> qui sanskritisa sa lignée, la faisant descendre de la Lune [<a href="#entry-candrava_mza" class="skt-link">candravaṃśa</a>]; les Candalās régnèrent du 9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ au 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle à <a href="#entry-Ukhajuraaho" class="skt-link">Khajurāho</a>, puis à Mahoba; ils étaient initialement vassaux des <a href="#entry-Upratiihaaraas" class="skt-link">Pratīhārās</a>; ils patronnèrent les arts et les lettres, et élevèrent de nombreux temples hindous et jaïns.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चन्दन</span> <i>candana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-cand" class="skt-link">cand</a>] <a href="#entry-candana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-candana" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. bot. <i>Sirium myrtifolium</i>, arbre ou bois de santal; on dit qu'il est aimé des serpents <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>sandalum</i>; ang. sandalwood; fr. santal.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candananagara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candananagara</i> [<a href="#entry-nagara" class="skt-link">nagara</a>] <a href="#entry-candananagara" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. du village de Candananagara au Bengale, sur le Gange à 30 km en amont de Calcutta, fondé comme comptoir commercial français (Chandernagor) par Duplessis en 1674, rattaché à l'Inde en 1950; mod. Candannagar.<br> <a href="#entry-candana" class="skt-link">candana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चन्द्र</span> <i>candra</i> [<a href="#entry-cand" class="skt-link">cand</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-candra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-candra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-candraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>candrā</i> brillant, qui illumine — <a href="#entry-candra" class="skt-link">m.</a> la Lune [«qui brille»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du dieu Candra «Brillant», personnifiant la Lune; en tant que <a href="#entry-Usoma" class="skt-link">Soma</a> il est le récipient de l'<a href="#entry-am_rta" class="skt-link">amṛta</a>; il est né du barattage de la mer de lait [<a href="#entry-k_siirodamathana" class="skt-link">kṣīrodamathana</a>], mais aussi des larmes d'<a href="#entry-Uatri" class="skt-link">Atri</a> comme fils d'<a href="#entry-Uanasuuyaa" class="skt-link">Anasūyā</a>, et il est donc dit doublement né [<a href="#entry-dvija" class="skt-link">dvija</a>]; il est le gardien [<a href="#entry-dikpaala" class="skt-link">dikpāla</a>] de la direction du Nord-Est; en tant qu'<a href="#entry-Uo_sadhipati" class="skt-link">Oṣadhipati</a> il règne sur les plantes; son char est attelé de 7 oies sacrées [<a href="#entry-ha_msa" class="skt-link">haṃsa</a>]; il épousa 27 filles de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>, identifiées aux astérismes ou maisons lunaires [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>];
