comme il les délaissait au profit de la seule <a href="#entry-Urohi_nii_1" class="skt-link">Rohiṇī_1</a>, elles se plaignirent à <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a> qui le maudit et le condamna au dépérissement; il implora <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>, qui commua la sentence en dépérissement périodique; il est le Roi des étoiles <a href="#entry-Utaarakaaraaja" class="skt-link">Tārakārāja</a>; après avoir effectué le sacrifice <a href="#entry-raajasuuya" class="skt-link">rājasūya</a> de consécration royale, il devint arrogant et enleva la femme <a href="#entry-Utaaraa" class="skt-link">Tārā</a> de <a href="#entry-Ub_rhaspati" class="skt-link">Bṛhaspati</a>; une guerre avec les dieux [<a href="#entry-taarakaamayayuddha" class="skt-link">tārakāmayayuddha</a>] s'ensuivit; <a href="#entry-Ucandra" class="skt-link">Candra</a> fut coupé en deux par le trident de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>, et dut rendre <a href="#entry-Utaaraa" class="skt-link">Tārā</a>, dont il avait obtenu comme fils <a href="#entry-Ubudha" class="skt-link">Budha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. l'une des 10 régions [<a href="#entry-dviipa" class="skt-link">dvīpa</a>] formant le <a href="#entry-jambudviipa" class="skt-link">jambudvīpa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. le nombre 1 <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>candragomin</i>] gram. école du grammarien <a href="#entry-Ucandragomii" class="skt-link">Candragomī</a> — ifc. le meilleur des.<br> <i>kṣīrodārṇavasaṃbhūta</i> <i>atrinetrasamudbhava</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>gṛhāṇārghyaṃ</i> <i>mayā</i> <i>dattaṃ</i> <i>rohiṇyā</i> <i>sahito</i> <i>śaśin</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (prononcé en faisant une offrande de lait à <a href="#entry-Ucandra" class="skt-link">Candra</a> lors de <a href="#entry-k_r_s_naa_s_tamii" class="skt-link">kṛṣṇāṣṭamī</a>) Ô toi produit de l'écume de la mer de lait, issu de l'oeil d'<a href="#entry-Uatri" class="skt-link">Atri</a>, toi qui porte le lièvre, accompagné de l'étoile rouge, accepte l'oblation que je t'offre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candra_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चन्द्रट</span> <i>candraṭa</i> <a href="#entry-candra_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> méd. np. de Candraṭa, médecin du 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, fils de <a href="#entry-Utiisa_ta" class="skt-link">Tīsaṭa</a>, dont il commenta la <a href="#entry-Ucikitsaakalikaa" class="skt-link">Cikitsākalikā</a>; on lui doit aussi la <a href="#entry-Usuzrutapaa_thazuddhi" class="skt-link">Suśrutapāṭhaśuddhi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candraaditya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candrāditya</i> [<a href="#entry-aaditya" class="skt-link">āditya</a>] <a href="#entry-candraaditya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Candrāditya, fils aîné du roi <a href="#entry-Ucaalukya" class="skt-link">Cālukya</a> <a href="#entry-Upulikezii_2" class="skt-link">Pulikeśī_2</a>; il combattit ses 4 frères; à sa mort, son épouse <a href="#entry-Uvijayabha_t_taarikaa" class="skt-link">Vijayabhaṭṭārikā</a> assura la régence de l'empire jusqu'à l'avènement de <a href="#entry-Uvikramaaditya_1" class="skt-link">Vikramāditya_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candraam_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candrāmṛta</i> [<a href="#entry-am_rta" class="skt-link">amṛta</a>] <a href="#entry-candraam_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> liquide humectant la cavité palatale; eau de jouvence.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candraananda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candrānanda</i> [<a href="#entry-aananda" class="skt-link">ānanda</a>] <a href="#entry-candraananda" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du philosophe Candrānanda (10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle), auteur de la <a href="#entry-Uvaize_sikasuutrav_rtti" class="skt-link">Vaiśeṣikasūtravṛtti</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candraapii_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candrāpīḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-aapii_da" class="skt-link">āpīḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-candraapii_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Candrāpīḍa «Auréolé de la Lune», fils de <a href="#entry-Ujanamejaya" class="skt-link">Janamejaya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [<a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>] np. du prince Candrāpīḍa_1, fils du roi <a href="#entry-Utaaraapii_da" class="skt-link">Tārāpīḍa</a> et de la reine <a href="#entry-Uvilaasavatii" class="skt-link">Vilāsavatī</a>; à 16 ans il est intronisé prince héritier, puis part conquérir le monde; il s'égare et trouve au lac <a href="#entry-Uacchoda" class="skt-link">Acchoda</a> la belle <a href="#entry-Umahaazvetaa" class="skt-link">Mahāśvetā</a> qui lui raconte ses amours malheureuses avec <a href="#entry-Upu_n_dariika_2" class="skt-link">Puṇḍarīka_2</a>, le suicide de celui-ci et sa montée au Ciel avec <a href="#entry-Ucandra" class="skt-link">Candra</a>; elle l'emmène au Mont <a href="#entry-Uhemakuu_ta" class="skt-link">Hemakūṭa</a> et l'introduit auprès de <a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>; ils sont pris de passion l'un envers l'autre; mais <a href="#entry-Ucandraapii_da_1" class="skt-link">Candrāpīḍa_1</a> doit retourner au palais sur l'ordre de son père, et ils sont séparés; il part à la recherche de son ami <a href="#entry-Uvaizampaayana_1" class="skt-link">Vaiśampāyana_1</a> et tombe dans une grande léthargie en apprenant sa triste fin; il est l'incarnation de <a href="#entry-Ucandra" class="skt-link">Candra</a> frappé de malédiction par <a href="#entry-Upu_n_dariika_2" class="skt-link">Puṇḍarīka_2</a> qui renaît comme le triste roi <a href="#entry-Uzuudraka_1" class="skt-link">Śūdraka_1</a>, alors que son ami <a href="#entry-Uvaizampaayana_1" class="skt-link">Vaiśampāyana_1</a> renaît comme le perroquet narrateur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candrabhaagaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candrabhāgā</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaaga" class="skt-link">bhāga</a>] <a href="#entry-candrabhaagaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. np. de la rivière Candrabhāgā au Pañjab (mod. Chenab, confluence de la Candrā et la Bhāgā) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>candrabhāgī</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candrabindu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candrabindu</i> [<a href="#entry-bindu" class="skt-link">bindu</a>] <a href="#entry-candrabindu" class="skt-link">m.</a> phon. signe de nasalisation, pour indiquer le changement d'une nasale muette en <a href="#entry-anusvaara" class="skt-link">anusvāra</a> devant une semivoyelle [<a href="#entry-anunaasika" class="skt-link">anunāsika</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candracuu_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candracūḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-cuu_daa" class="skt-link">cūḍā</a>] <a href="#entry-candracuu_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [«qui porte la lune comme crête»] épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-candramauli" class="skt-link">candramauli</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candragomin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candragomin</i> [<a href="#entry-gomin" class="skt-link">gomin</a>] <a href="#entry-candragomin" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du lettré bengali bd. Candragomī (vers le 5⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); il est l'auteur de la grammaire <a href="#entry-Ucaandravyaakara_na" class="skt-link">Cāndravyākaraṇa</a>, de la pièce de théâtre [<a href="#entry-naa_taka" class="skt-link">nāṭaka</a>] <a href="#entry-Ulokaananda" class="skt-link">Lokānanda</a>, et de nombreux autres ouvrages.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candragraha_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candragrahaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-graha_na" class="skt-link">grahaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-candragraha_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> éclipse de lune.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candragupta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candragupta</i> [<a href="#entry-gupta" class="skt-link">gupta</a>] <a href="#entry-candragupta" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du roi Candragupta <a href="#entry-Umaurya" class="skt-link">Maurya</a>, général rebelle qui renversa la dynastie des <a href="#entry-Unanda" class="skt-link">Nanda</a> au <a href="#entry-Umagadha" class="skt-link">Magadha</a> et fonda la dynastie <a href="#entry-Umaurya" class="skt-link">Maurya</a> en 321 ant.; sa cour se tenait à <a href="#entry-Upaa_taliputra" class="skt-link">Pāṭaliputra</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukau_tilya" class="skt-link">Kauṭilya</a> était son mentor et premier ministre; il est connu des Grecs comme Sandrakottos, et Plutarque dit (selon l'historien grec Mégasthènes) qu'il rencontra Alexandre au Pañjab en 329 ant.; après avoir conquis un empire s'étendant jusqu'au <a href="#entry-Ugandhaara" class="skt-link">Gandhāra</a>, il devint renonçant <a href="#entry-jaina" class="skt-link">jaina</a>, et abdiqua en faveur de son fils <a href="#entry-Ubindusaara" class="skt-link">Bindusāra</a>, père de l'empereur <a href="#entry-Uazoka" class="skt-link">Aśoka</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ucaa_nakya" class="skt-link">Cāṇakya</a>, <a href="#entry-Umayuurapo_saka" class="skt-link">Mayūrapoṣaka</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. du roi Candragupta_1, premier véritable empereur de la dynastie <a href="#entry-Ugupta" class="skt-link">Gupta</a>; il se donna le titre de <a href="#entry-Umahaaraajaadhiraaja" class="skt-link">Mahārājādhirāja</a>; son accession au trône en 320 marque le début de l'ère [<a href="#entry-sa_mvat" class="skt-link">saṃvat</a>] <a href="#entry-Ugupta" class="skt-link">Gupta</a>; son fils <a href="#entry-Usamudragupta" class="skt-link">Samudragupta</a> lui succéda <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> np. du roi <a href="#entry-Ukumaara" class="skt-link">Kumāra</a> Candragupta, fils cadet de <a href="#entry-Usamudragupta" class="skt-link">Samudragupta</a>; il succéda à son frère aîné <a href="#entry-Uraamagupta" class="skt-link">Rāmagupta</a> après l'avoir assassiné, et régna comme Candragupta_2 (~380–415); cf. <a href="#entry-Uvikramaaditya" class="skt-link">Vikramāditya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candrahaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candrahāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-haasa" class="skt-link">hāsa</a>] <a href="#entry-candrahaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de l'épée Candrahāsa «Rire de Lune», que <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> donna à <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a> pour le récompenser de ses 1000 ans d'austérités [<a href="#entry-tapas" class="skt-link">tapas</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candrajanaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candrajanaka</i> [<a href="#entry-janaka" class="skt-link">janaka</a>] <a href="#entry-candrajanaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> np. de Candrajanaka, épith. de l'océan «où est né <a href="#entry-Ucandra" class="skt-link">Candra</a>».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चन्द्रक</span> <i>candraka</i> [<a href="#entry-candra" class="skt-link">candra</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-candraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-candraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-candrikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>candrikā</i> semblable à la lune — <a href="#entry-candraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> œil de plume de paon; ongle — <a href="#entry-candraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> poivre — f. cf. <a href="#entry-candrikaa" class="skt-link">candrikā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candrakaanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candrakānta</i> [<a href="#entry-kaanta" class="skt-link">kānta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-candrakaanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-candrakaanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-candrakaantaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>candrakāntā</i> cher à la lune <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> charmant comme la lune — <a href="#entry-candrakaanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> pierre de lune, une gemme [<a href="#entry-ma_ni" class="skt-link">maṇi</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. bot. <i>Nymphæa esculenta</i>, nymphéa blanc (qui fleurit la nuit); syn. <a href="#entry-kumuda" class="skt-link">kumuda</a> — <a href="#entry-candrakaanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> bois de santal — <a href="#entry-candrakaantaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>candrakāntā</i> nuit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candrakaanti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candrakānti</i> [<a href="#entry-kaanti" class="skt-link">kānti</a>] <a href="#entry-candrakaanti" class="skt-link">f.</a> lustre de la lune; lumière de lune <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> disque de la lune au 9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candrakalaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candrakalā</i> [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>] <a href="#entry-candrakalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> seizième partie du cercle lunaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [tantr.] l'une des 16 divinités féminines associées <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. croissant de lune un jour avant ou après la nouvelle lune <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> marque en forme de croissant sur l'ongle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candraketu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candraketu</i> [<a href="#entry-ketu" class="skt-link">ketu</a>] <a href="#entry-candraketu" class="skt-link">m.</a> np. de prince Candraketu «Qui a la lune sur sa bannière» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Candraketu_1, fils de <a href="#entry-Ulak_sma_na" class="skt-link">Lakṣmaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candrakiirti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candrakīrti</i> [<a href="#entry-kiirti" class="skt-link">kīrti</a>] <a href="#entry-candrakiirti" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du philosophe bd. Candrakīrti, commentateur de <a href="#entry-Unaagaarjuna" class="skt-link">Nāgārjuna</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Candrakīrti_1 «Gloire de l'astre Lune», prince d'<a href="#entry-Uujjayinii" class="skt-link">Ujjayinī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candrama_n_dala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candramaṇḍala</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_n_dala" class="skt-link">maṇḍala</a>] <a href="#entry-candrama_n_dala" class="skt-link">n.</a> disque de la lune.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candramaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candramāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-maasa" class="skt-link">māsa</a>] <a href="#entry-candramaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. mois lunaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candramas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candramas</i> [<a href="#entry-maas" class="skt-link">mās</a>] <a href="#entry-candramas" class="skt-link">m.</a> lune <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Candramā «Lune», épith. du roi Samudragupta, fils de <a href="#entry-Ucandragupta_1" class="skt-link">Candragupta_1</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Uvikramaaditya" class="skt-link">Vikramāditya</a> — <a href="#entry-candramasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>candramasī</i> myth. np. de Candramasī, épith. de <a href="#entry-Utaaraa" class="skt-link">Tārā</a> «Ravie par <a href="#entry-Ucandra" class="skt-link">Candra</a>».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candramauli">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candramauli</i> [<a href="#entry-mauli" class="skt-link">mauli</a>] <a href="#entry-candramauli" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [«qui a la lune pour diadème»] épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-candracuu_da" class="skt-link">candracūḍa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candramauliizvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candramaulīśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-candramauliizvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de Candramaulīśvara, <a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a> de cristal du sanctuaire de <a href="#entry-Ucidambara" class="skt-link">Cidambara</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. de Candramaulīśvara_1, temple dédié à <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> à <a href="#entry-Uu_dupii" class="skt-link">Uḍupī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-candramauli" class="skt-link">candramauli</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candramukha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candramukha</i> [<a href="#entry-mukha" class="skt-link">mukha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-candramukha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-candramukha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-candramukhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>candramukhī</i> qui a une face de lune.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candramuku_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candramukuṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-muku_ta" class="skt-link">mukuṭa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-candramuku_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-candramuku_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-candramuku_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>candramukuṭā</i> myth. np. de Candramukuṭa, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «Qui a la lune comme diadème».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candranaa_dii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candranāḍī</i> [<a href="#entry-naa_dii" class="skt-link">nāḍī</a>] <a href="#entry-candranaa_dii" class="skt-link">f.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-i_daa" class="skt-link">iḍā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candraprabha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candraprabha</i> [<a href="#entry-prabha" class="skt-link">prabha</a>] <a href="#entry-candraprabha" class="skt-link">m.</a> jn. np. de Candraprabha «Lumière de Lune», 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-tiirthafkara" class="skt-link">tīrthaṅkara</a> mythique; son corps est blanc, son symbole est un croissant de lune.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candrasi_mha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candrasiṃha</i> [<a href="#entry-si_mha" class="skt-link">siṃha</a>] <a href="#entry-candrasi_mha" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du <a href="#entry-pa_n_dita" class="skt-link">paṇḍita</a> Candrasiṃha, auteur du <a href="#entry-Upadak_rtya" class="skt-link">Padakṛtya</a> (17⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candrava_mza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candravaṃśa</i> [<a href="#entry-va_mza" class="skt-link">vaṃśa</a>] <a href="#entry-candrava_mza" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-somava_mza" class="skt-link">somavaṃśa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candrazekhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candraśekhara</i> [<a href="#entry-zekhara" class="skt-link">śekhara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-candrazekhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-candrazekhara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-candrazekharaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>candraśekharā</i> myth. np. de Candraśekhara, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «Qui a la lune comme diadème».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candrikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चन्द्रिका</span> <i>candrikā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-candraka" class="skt-link">candraka</a>] <a href="#entry-candrikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> clair de lune — ifc. splendeur, clarté de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. éclaircissement sur (nom d'ouvrages) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>tātparyacandrikā</i>] lit. np. de la Candrikā de <a href="#entry-Uvyaasatiirtha" class="skt-link">Vyāsatīrtha</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Utaatparyacandrikaa" class="skt-link">Tātparyacandrikā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candrikaacaarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candrikācārya</i> [<a href="#entry-aacaarya" class="skt-link">ācārya</a>] <a href="#entry-candrikaacaarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Candrikācārya «Maître de la <a href="#entry-Ucandrikaa" class="skt-link">Candrikā</a>», titre donné à <a href="#entry-Uvyaasatiirtha" class="skt-link">Vyāsatīrtha</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-candrikaa" class="skt-link">candrikā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-candrodaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>candrodaya</i> [<a href="#entry-udaya" class="skt-link">udaya</a>] <a href="#entry-candrodaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lever de lune.<br> <a href="#entry-candra" class="skt-link">candra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-capala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चपल</span> <i>capala</i> [<a href="#entry-kamp" class="skt-link">kamp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-capala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-capala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-capalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>capalā</i> mobile, agile, instable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hésitant; capricieux, changeant; timide <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> inconstant, étourdi; espiègle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-capalataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>capalatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-capalataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> inconstance; étourderie.<br> <a href="#entry-capala" class="skt-link">capala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cape_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चपेट</span> <i>capeṭa</i> <a href="#entry-cape_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-cape_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>capeṭā</i> gifle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-car">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">चर्</span> <i>car</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(carati)</i> pr. md. <i>(carate)</i> pft. <i>(cacāra)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] <i>(acārīt)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <i>(acīcarat)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>carita</i><i>)</i> ppr. <i>(carat)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ati</i>, <i>anu</i>, <i>apa</i>, <i>abhi</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>ut</i>, <i>upa</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> aller, marcher, déambuler; paître <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> parcourir, suivre (un chemin); vivre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se mettre à, se livrer à, s'occuper de, exercer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> accomplir, commettre, faire; agir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> amener qqn. ⟨acc.⟩ à ⟨acc.⟩ — ca. <i>(cārayati)</i> faire mouvoir, faire pratiquer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> faire reconnaître par un espion ⟨i.⟩ — ca. md. <i>(cārayate)</i> réfléchir, hésiter, avoir des doutes sur ⟨loc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. κολος; lat. <i>curro</i>, <i>carrus</i>; fr. char; ang. car.<br> <i>bhaikṣaṃ</i> <i>car</i> demander l'aumône; mendier.<br> <i>mārgaṃ</i> <i>car</i> se frayer un chemin.<br> <i>mithunaṃ</i> <i>car</i> avoir des rapports sexuels avec qqn. ⟨i.⟩.<br> <i>mṛgayāṃ</i> <i>car</i> aller à la chasse.<br> <i>vrataṃ</i> <i>car</i> observer un vœu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-car_sa_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चर्षणि</span> <i>carṣaṇi</i> [<a href="#entry-k_r_s" class="skt-link">kṛṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-car_sa_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-car_sa_ni" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-car_sa_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>carṣaṇī</i> actif, industrieux; agile, doué — <a href="#entry-car_sa_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>carṣaṇī</i> myth. np. de Carṣaṇī «la Semeuse», épouse de <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a> et mère de <a href="#entry-Ubh_rgu" class="skt-link">Bhṛgu</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>carṣaṇyas</i> les sédentaires, les agriculteurs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चर</span> <i>cara</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-car" class="skt-link">car</a>] a. <a href="#entry-cara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-cara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-carii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>carī</i> mobile; qui bouge — ifc. qui va dans, qui habite; qu'on envoie — <a href="#entry-cara" class="skt-link">m.</a> émissaire, espion; vent, souffle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चरण</span> <i>caraṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-car" class="skt-link">car</a>] <a href="#entry-cara_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-cara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> pied; jambe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> section, division <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. stance; syn. <a href="#entry-paada" class="skt-link">pāda</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. école védique — <a href="#entry-cara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> conduite, manière d'être, pratique; accomplissement.<br> <i>guroś</i> <i>caraṇaṃ</i> <i>hariḥ</i> <i>sāranam</i> Pratique de <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a>, protection de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> (<a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a> post-<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cara_nadaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caraṇadāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-daasa" class="skt-link">dāsa</a>] <a href="#entry-cara_nadaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du philosophe Caraṇadāsa, auteur not. du <a href="#entry-Uguruzi_syasa_mvaada" class="skt-link">Guruśiṣyasaṃvāda</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-cara_na" class="skt-link">caraṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caraacara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>carācara</i> [<a href="#entry-acara" class="skt-link">acara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caraacara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caraacara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caraacaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>carācarā</i> [«le mobile et l'immobile»] toutes les créatures; l'univers.<br> <a href="#entry-cara" class="skt-link">cara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चरक</span> <i>caraka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-car" class="skt-link">car</a>] <a href="#entry-caraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> étudiant nomade; ascète, moine errant; espion <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Caraka, fils d'un <a href="#entry-muni" class="skt-link">muni</a>; il est l'incarnation de <a href="#entry-Uze_sa" class="skt-link">Śeṣa</a> pris de pitié par une visite sur Terre où il constata les ravages des maladies; il fit la synthèse des connaissances médicales d'<a href="#entry-Uagniveza" class="skt-link">Agniveśa</a> et des autres disciples d'<a href="#entry-Uaatreya" class="skt-link">Ātreya</a> dans la <a href="#entry-Ucarakasa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">Carakasaṃhitā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-carakasa_mhitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>carakasaṃhitā</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">saṃhitā</a>] <a href="#entry-carakasa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Carakasaṃhitā, traité de médecine [<a href="#entry-Uaayurveda" class="skt-link">Āyurveda</a>] attribué à <a href="#entry-Ucaraka" class="skt-link">Caraka</a>; il fut sans doute composé entre 100 et 400, et révisé par <a href="#entry-Ud_r_dhabala" class="skt-link">Dṛḍhabala</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-caraka" class="skt-link">caraka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-carama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चरम</span> <i>carama</i> [super. <a href="#entry-cara" class="skt-link">cara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-carama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-carama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caramaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caramā</i> dernier, final.<br> <i>caramā</i> <i>kriyā</i> cérémonie funéraire.<br> <i>caramaṃ</i> <i>kim</i> et quoi encore?
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caramazloka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caramaśloka</i> [<a href="#entry-zloka" class="skt-link">śloka</a>] <a href="#entry-caramazloka" class="skt-link">m.</a> épitaphe.<br> <a href="#entry-carama" class="skt-link">carama</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-carat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चरत्</span> <i>carat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-car" class="skt-link">car</a>] a. <a href="#entry-carat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-carat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-carantii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>carantī</i> marchant, allant, errant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-carca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चर्च</span> <i>carca</i> <a href="#entry-carca" class="skt-link">m.</a> discussion, délibération — <a href="#entry-carcaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>carcā</i> préoccupation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> répétition (not. en récitant le <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> discussion; investigation — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(carcayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>carcita</i><i>)</i> répéter (un mot, not. en récitant le <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a>); discuter, délibérer — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(carcayate)</i> étudier.<br> <i>carcāṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> être préoccupé par, se soucier de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-carcaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चर्चा</span> <i>carcā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-carca" class="skt-link">carca</a>] <a href="#entry-carcaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> récitation avec répétition du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> (not. en insérant <a href="#entry-iti" class="skt-link">iti</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-carcaapaa_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>carcāpāṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-paa_tha" class="skt-link">pāṭha</a>] <a href="#entry-carcaapaa_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. récitation du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> à la manière <a href="#entry-carcaa" class="skt-link">carcā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-carcaapaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>carcāpāra</i> [<a href="#entry-paara" class="skt-link">pāra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-carcaapaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-carcaapaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-carcaapaarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>carcāpārī</i> qui étudie à fond le <a href="#entry-carcaapaa_tha" class="skt-link">carcāpāṭha</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-carcaa" class="skt-link">carcā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-carcita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चर्चित</span> <i>carcita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-carca" class="skt-link">carca</a>] a. <a href="#entry-carcita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-carcita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-carcitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>carcitā</i> répété.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-carita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चरित</span> <i>carita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-car" class="skt-link">car</a>] a. <a href="#entry-carita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-carita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caritaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caritā</i> accompli; pratiqué — <a href="#entry-carita" class="skt-link">n.</a> action accomplie, chose faite; passé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> conduite, comportement, manière d'agir; pratique, exercice d'une fonction; présent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ensemble des actes, vie; existence; exploits, aventures <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ang. care; fr. charité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caritaam_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caritāmṛta</i> [<a href="#entry-am_rta" class="skt-link">amṛta</a>] <a href="#entry-caritaam_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. [«nectar des actes de»] nom de biographies.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caritaartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caritārtha</i> [<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caritaartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caritaartha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caritaarthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caritārthā</i> qui a atteint son but, qui a réussi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caritaarthataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caritārthatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-caritaarthataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> succès.<br> <a href="#entry-caritaartha" class="skt-link">caritārtha</a> <br> <a href="#entry-carita" class="skt-link">carita</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caritra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चरित्र</span> <i>caritra</i> [instr. <a href="#entry-car" class="skt-link">car</a>] <a href="#entry-caritra" class="skt-link">n.</a> conduite, action, pratique; œuvre; saga, aventures, exploits.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caritrabandhaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caritrabandhaka</i> [<a href="#entry-bandhaka" class="skt-link">bandhaka</a>] <a href="#entry-caritrabandhaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caritrabandhaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> prêt; gage, hypothèque.<br> <a href="#entry-caritra" class="skt-link">caritra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-carma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चर्म</span> <i>carma</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-carman" class="skt-link">carman</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-carmad_r_s_ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>carmadṛṣṭi</i> [<a href="#entry-d_r_s_ti" class="skt-link">dṛṣṭi</a>] <a href="#entry-carmad_r_s_ti" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. connaissance superficielle de l'apparence des êtres et des choses; opp. <a href="#entry-aatmad_r_s_ti" class="skt-link">ātmadṛṣṭi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-carmada_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>carmadaṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-da_n_da" class="skt-link">daṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-carmada_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> fouet.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-carmaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>carmaja</i> [<a href="#entry-ja" class="skt-link">ja</a>] a. <a href="#entry-carmaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-carmaja" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-carmajaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>carmajā</i> fait de cuir — <a href="#entry-carmaja" class="skt-link">n.</a> poil de la peau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-carmakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>carmakāra</i> [<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-carmakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> corroyeur, cordonnier, travailleur du cuir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. homme de basse classe, not. fils d'une femme <a href="#entry-ca_n_daalaa" class="skt-link">caṇḍālā</a> ou <a href="#entry-vaidehii" class="skt-link">vaidehī</a> ou <a href="#entry-ni_saadii" class="skt-link">niṣādī</a> — <a href="#entry-carmakaarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>carmakārī</i> femme de travailleur du cuir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hi. <i>camār</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-carmamu_n_daa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>carmamuṇḍā</i> [<a href="#entry-mu_n_daa" class="skt-link">muṇḍā</a>] <a href="#entry-carmamu_n_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-caamu_n_daa" class="skt-link">cāmuṇḍā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-carman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चर्मन्</span> <i>carman</i> <a href="#entry-carman" class="skt-link">n.</a> peau, cuir; bouclier (de cuir) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. peau d'antilope d'un <a href="#entry-brahmacaarin" class="skt-link">brahmacārin</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-carmarafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>carmaraṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-rafga" class="skt-link">raṅga</a>] a. <a href="#entry-carmarafga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-carmarafga" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-carmarafgaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>carmaraṅgā</i> qui teint le cuir — <a href="#entry-carmarafgaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>carmaraṅgā</i> natu. bot. <i>Cassia auriculata</i>, arbuste buissonnant aux feuilles composées, aux fleurs jaune vif en corymbes, utilisé en tannerie et en médecine; syn. <a href="#entry-aavartakii" class="skt-link">āvartakī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-carma" class="skt-link">carma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चरु</span> <i>caru</i> <a href="#entry-caru" class="skt-link">m.</a> oblation constituée d'une bouillie de riz, orge et pois cuits dans du lait <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> le chaudron servant à sa préparation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-carya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चर्य</span> <i>carya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-car" class="skt-link">car</a>] a. <a href="#entry-carya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-carya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caryaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caryā</i> qui doit être pratiqué ou accompli — <a href="#entry-carya" class="skt-link">n.</a> ifc. transport par — f. cf. <a href="#entry-caryaa" class="skt-link">caryā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caryaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चर्या</span> <i>caryā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-durga" class="skt-link">durga</a>] <a href="#entry-caryaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> marche, voyage, moyen de locomotion <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> conduite, occupation; pratique, observance (de ⟨i. iic.⟩); not. observance de règles monastiques et de rites religieux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> office, service, ministère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caryaacaryavinizcaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caryācaryaviniścaya</i> [<a href="#entry-aacarya" class="skt-link">ācarya</a>-<a href="#entry-vinizcaya" class="skt-link">viniścaya</a>] <a href="#entry-caryaacaryavinizcaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. np. du Caryācaryaviniścaya, recueil de poèmes mystiques [<a href="#entry-caryaagiiti" class="skt-link">caryāgīti</a>] en bengali ancien; syn. <a href="#entry-caryaapaada" class="skt-link">caryāpāda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caryaagiiti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caryāgīti</i> [<a href="#entry-giiti" class="skt-link">gīti</a>] <a href="#entry-caryaagiiti" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. poème mystique chanté dans la tradition tantr. bengalie <a href="#entry-sahajayaana" class="skt-link">sahajayāna</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ucaryaacaryavinizcaya" class="skt-link">Caryācaryaviniścaya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caryaamelaapakapradiipa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caryāmelāpakapradīpa</i> [<a href="#entry-melaapaka" class="skt-link">melāpaka</a>-<a href="#entry-pradiipa" class="skt-link">pradīpa</a>] <a href="#entry-caryaamelaapakapradiipa" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Caryāmelāpakapradīpa «Explication intégrant la pratique», ouvrage bd. [<a href="#entry-Uvajraayana" class="skt-link">Vajrāyana</a>] d'<a href="#entry-Uaaryadeva" class="skt-link">Āryadeva</a>; il est le fondement de l'école ésotérique <a href="#entry-guhyasamaaja" class="skt-link">guhyasamāja</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-caryaa" class="skt-link">caryā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caryaapaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caryāpāda</i> [<a href="#entry-paada" class="skt-link">pāda</a>] <a href="#entry-caryaapaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. ensemble de 47 poèmes mystiques [<a href="#entry-caryaagiiti" class="skt-link">caryāgīti</a>] en bengali exprimant l'expérience mystique en bd. tantr. <a href="#entry-sahajayaana" class="skt-link">sahajayāna</a>, composés entre le 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ et le 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècles.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">चत्</span> <i>cat</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(catati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>catta</i><i>)</i> ppr. <i>(catat)</i> partir, se cacher — ca. <i>(cātayati)</i> faire partir, chasser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catas_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चतसृ</span> <i>catasṛ</i> [f. <a href="#entry-catur" class="skt-link">catur</a>] num. <a href="#entry-catas_r" class="skt-link">*</a> pl. quatre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catu_h">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चतुः</span> <i>catuḥ</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-catur" class="skt-link">catur</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catu_h_sa_s_ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>catuḥṣaṣṭi</i> var. <i>catuṣṣaṣṭi</i> [<a href="#entry-_sa_s_ti" class="skt-link">ṣaṣṭi</a>] <a href="#entry-catu_h_sa_s_ti" class="skt-link">f.</a> 64.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catu_h_sa_s_tikalaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>catuḥṣaṣṭikalā</i> var. <i>catuṣṣaṣṭikalā</i> [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>] <a href="#entry-catu_h_sa_s_tikalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> pl. <i>catuḥṣaṣṭikalās</i> soc. les 64 arts: le <a href="#entry-Ukaamasuutra" class="skt-link">Kāmasūtra</a> énumère: le chant [<a href="#entry-giita" class="skt-link">gīta</a>], la musique [<a href="#entry-vaadya" class="skt-link">vādya</a>], la danse [<a href="#entry-n_rtya" class="skt-link">nṛtya</a>] et le théâtre [<a href="#entry-naa_tya" class="skt-link">nāṭya</a>], le dessin, la peinture et la calligraphie [<a href="#entry-aalekhya" class="skt-link">ālekhya</a>], l'ornement du visage et du corps [<a href="#entry-vize_sakacchedya" class="skt-link">viśeṣakacchedya</a>], l'ornement des autels avec du riz et des fleurs [<a href="#entry-ta_n_dulakusumavalivikaara" class="skt-link">taṇḍulakusumavalivikāra</a>], l'arrangement de jonchées de fleurs [<a href="#entry-pu_spaastara_na" class="skt-link">puṣpāstaraṇa</a>], la coloration des dents, des membres et des vêtements [<a href="#entry-dazanavasanaafgaraaga" class="skt-link">daśanavasanāṅgarāga</a>], la confection de mosaïques en pierres précieuses [<a href="#entry-ma_nibhuumikaakarma" class="skt-link">maṇibhūmikākarma</a>], l'arrangement du lit [<a href="#entry-zayanaracana" class="skt-link">śayanaracana</a>], la musique avec un pot rempli d'eau [<a href="#entry-udakavaadya" class="skt-link">udakavādya</a>], le jeu dans l'eau [<a href="#entry-udakaaghaata" class="skt-link">udakāghāta</a>], les diverses [<a href="#entry-citra" class="skt-link">citra</a>] sortes de <a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a> magique, la confection des guirlandes [<a href="#entry-maalyagrathanavikalpa" class="skt-link">mālyagrathanavikalpa</a>], l'arrangement de diadèmes [<a href="#entry-zekharaapii_dayojana" class="skt-link">śekharāpīḍayojana</a>] et de fleurs dans les oreilles et les cheveux l'art de la toilette [<a href="#entry-nepathyaprayoga" class="skt-link">nepathyaprayoga</a>], l'ornement des oreilles [<a href="#entry-kar_napattrabhafga" class="skt-link">karṇapattrabhaṅga</a>], l'art du parfum et des cosmétiques [<a href="#entry-gandhayukti" class="skt-link">gandhayukti</a>], l'art de la parure [<a href="#entry-bhuu_sa_nayojana" class="skt-link">bhūṣaṇayojana</a>], la jonglerie et l'art du cirque [<a href="#entry-aindrajaala" class="skt-link">aindrajāla</a>], l'art du déguisement [<a href="#entry-kaucumaarayoga" class="skt-link">kaucumārayoga</a>], la prestidigitation [<a href="#entry-hastalaaghava" class="skt-link">hastalāghava</a>], l'art de préparer les mets [<a href="#entry-vicitrazaakayuu_sabhak_syavikaarakriyaa" class="skt-link">vicitraśākayūṣabhakṣyavikārakriyā</a>], l'art de préparer les boissons [<a href="#entry-paanakarasaraagaasavayojana" class="skt-link">pānakarasarāgāsavayojana</a>], les travaux de couture [<a href="#entry-suuciivaanakarmaa_ni" class="skt-link">sūcīvānakarmāṇi</a>], l'art des marionnettes [<a href="#entry-suutrakrii_daa" class="skt-link">sūtrakrīḍā</a>], le luth et le tambourin [<a href="#entry-vii_naa_damaruvaadyaani" class="skt-link">vīṇāḍamaruvādyāni</a>], l'art de la devinette [<a href="#entry-prahelikaa" class="skt-link">prahelikā</a>], l'art de réciter les vers à rebours [<a href="#entry-pratimaalaa" class="skt-link">pratimālā</a>], l'art de prononcer des mots difficiles [<a href="#entry-durvaacakayoga" class="skt-link">durvācakayoga</a>], l'art de déchiffrer les manuscrits [<a href="#entry-pustakavaacana" class="skt-link">pustakavācana</a>], le théâtre et les contes [<a href="#entry-naa_takaakhyaayikaadarzana" class="skt-link">nāṭakākhyāyikādarśana</a>], le jeu de complétion de poèmes [<a href="#entry-kaavyasamasyaapuura_na" class="skt-link">kāvyasamasyāpūraṇa</a>], le tressage et le cannage [<a href="#entry-pa_t_tikaavetravaa_navikalpa" class="skt-link">paṭṭikāvetravāṇavikalpa</a>], les travaux de filage [<a href="#entry-tarkukarmaa_ni" class="skt-link">tarkukarmāṇi</a>], la menuiserie [<a href="#entry-tak_sa_na" class="skt-link">takṣaṇa</a>], l'architecture [<a href="#entry-vaastuvidyaa" class="skt-link">vāstuvidyā</a>], l'appréciation des monnaies et des pierres précieuses [<a href="#entry-ruupyaratnapariik_saa" class="skt-link">rūpyaratnaparīkṣā</a>], la métallurgie et l'alchimie [<a href="#entry-dhaatuvaada" class="skt-link">dhātuvāda</a>], la connaissance des gemmes [<a href="#entry-ma_niraagaakaraj_naana" class="skt-link">maṇirāgākarajñāna</a>], l'horticulture [<a href="#entry-v_rk_saayurvedayoga" class="skt-link">vṛkṣāyurvedayoga</a>], l'organisation de combat d'animaux [<a href="#entry-me_sakukku_talaavakayuddhavidhi" class="skt-link">meṣakukkuṭalāvakayuddhavidhi</a>], le dressage des perroquets et des mainates [<a href="#entry-zukasaarikaapralaapana" class="skt-link">śukasārikāpralāpana</a>], l'art de frotter, masser et friser les cheveux (<i>utsādane</i> <i>saṃvāhane</i> <i>keśamardane</i> <i>ca</i> <i>kauśalam</i>), l'art de parler avec ses doigts [<a href="#entry-ak_saramu_s_tikaakathana" class="skt-link">akṣaramuṣṭikākathana</a>], la connaissance des langues étrangères [<a href="#entry-mlecchitavikalpa" class="skt-link">mlecchitavikalpa</a>], la maîtrise des dialectes régionaux [<a href="#entry-dezabhaa_saavij_naana" class="skt-link">deśabhāṣāvijñāna</a>], l'arrangement d'un chariot de fleurs [<a href="#entry-pu_spazaka_tikaa" class="skt-link">puṣpaśakaṭikā</a>], la connaissance des diagrammes magiques et des amulettes [<a href="#entry-yantramaat_rkaa" class="skt-link">yantramātṛkā</a>], la mnémotechnie [<a href="#entry-dhaara_namaat_rkaa" class="skt-link">dhāraṇamātṛkā</a>], la déclamation du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> en cœur [<a href="#entry-sampaa_thya" class="skt-link">saṃpāṭhya</a>], la poésie mentale (<i>mānasī</i> <i>kāvyakriyā</i>), la lexicographie [<a href="#entry-abhidhaanakoza" class="skt-link">abhidhānakośa</a>], la prosodie [<a href="#entry-chandoj_naana" class="skt-link">chandojñāna</a>], la rhétorique [<a href="#entry-kriyaavikalpa" class="skt-link">kriyāvikalpa</a>], l'art des tours de magie [<a href="#entry-chalitakayogaas" class="skt-link">chalitakayogās</a>], l'art de dissimuler dans ses vêtements [<a href="#entry-vastragopana" class="skt-link">vastragopana</a>], les jeux de hasard [<a href="#entry-dyuutavize_sa" class="skt-link">dyūtaviśeṣa</a>], le jeu de dé [<a href="#entry-aakar_sakrii_daa" class="skt-link">ākarṣakrīḍā</a>], les jeux d'enfants [<a href="#entry-baalakrii_danakaani" class="skt-link">bālakrīḍanakāni</a>], et les connaissances militaires de la discipline [<a href="#entry-vainayikii" class="skt-link">vainayikī</a>], de la stratégie [<a href="#entry-vaijayikii" class="skt-link">vaijayikī</a>], et de la culture physique [<a href="#entry-vyaayaamikii" class="skt-link">vyāyāmikī</a>]; d'autres sources mentionnent d'autres arts, comme la science du vol [<a href="#entry-cauravidyaa" class="skt-link">cauravidyā</a>].<br> <i>catuḥṣaṣṭikalāgamaprayogacaturaḥ</i> [<a href="#entry-Udazakumaaracarita" class="skt-link">Daśakumāracarita</a>] Doué en l'acquisition et la pratique des 64 arts.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catu_h_sa_s_tiyoginii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>catuḥṣaṣṭiyoginī</i> var. <i>catuṣṣaṣṭiyoginī</i> [<a href="#entry-yoginii" class="skt-link">yoginī</a>] <a href="#entry-catu_h_sa_s_tiyoginii" class="skt-link">f.</a> pl. phil. les 64 puissances divines; elles sont représentées, associées à <a href="#entry-Ubhairava" class="skt-link">Bhairava</a> et <a href="#entry-Ukaatyaayanii" class="skt-link">Kātyāyanī</a>, dans des temples circulaires à ciel ouvert (vers 9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).<br> <a href="#entry-catu_h_sa_s_ti" class="skt-link">catuḥṣaṣṭi</a> <br> <a href="#entry-catu_h" class="skt-link">catuḥ</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catu_hzaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>catuḥśāla</i> [<a href="#entry-zaalaa" class="skt-link">śālā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-catu_hzaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-catu_hzaala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-catu_hzaalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>catuḥśālā</i> arch. (bâtiment) à 4 salles — <a href="#entry-catu_hzaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> maison traditionnelle du <a href="#entry-Ukerala" class="skt-link">Kerala</a>, à 4 corps de bâtiment.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catu_hzata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>catuḥśata</i> [<a href="#entry-zata" class="skt-link">śata</a>] <a href="#entry-catu_hzata" class="skt-link">n.</a> véd. 104 — a. <a href="#entry-catu_hzata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-catu_hzata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-catu_hzataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>catuḥśatā</i> 400 — <a href="#entry-catu_hzatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>catuḥśatī</i> lit. np. de la Catuḥśatī, poème tantrique «en 400 strophes».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catu_hzataka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>catuḥśataka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-catu_hzataka" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Catuḥśataka, poème philosophique en 400 strophes [<a href="#entry-kaarikaas" class="skt-link">kārikās</a>] d'<a href="#entry-Uaaryadeva" class="skt-link">Āryadeva</a>; il défend la doctrine de <a href="#entry-Unaagaarjuna" class="skt-link">Nāgārjuna</a> contre les autres écoles bd. et brahmaniques; il forme la base philosophique de l'école bd. <a href="#entry-Umahaayaana" class="skt-link">Mahāyāna</a> — <a href="#entry-catu_hzatikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>catuḥśatikā</i> var. <i>catuḥśatī</i> lit. id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catu_s_taya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>catuṣṭaya</i> [<a href="#entry--taya" class="skt-link">-taya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-catu_s_taya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-catu_s_taya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-catu_s_tayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>catuṣṭayī</i> quadruple, quadripartite — <a href="#entry-catu_s_taya" class="skt-link">n.</a> tétrade <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> les quatre à la fois.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catu_s_tva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>catuṣṭva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-catu_s_tva" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait d'être quadruple.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catu_ska">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>catuṣka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-catu_ska" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-catu_ska" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-catu_skaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>catuṣkā</i> quadripartite; carré <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> consistant en 4 éléments; not. <a href="#entry-svastika" class="skt-link">svastika</a> — <a href="#entry-catu_ska" class="skt-link">n.</a> quadruple <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> croisement de deux routes, carrefour <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. carré d'honneur tracé pour recevoir un hôte <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> collier à 4 rangs — a. <a href="#entry-catu_ska" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-catu_ska" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-catu_skii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>catuṣkī</i> étang quadrangulaire; moustiquaire; collier à 4 rangs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catu_sko_ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>catuṣkoṭi</i> [<a href="#entry-ko_ti" class="skt-link">koṭi</a>] <a href="#entry-catu_sko_ti" class="skt-link">f.</a> bd. principe logique en 4 parties, exprimant la totalité de la somme disjointe des quatre classes A et non B, non A et B, A et B, et non A et non B.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catu_spad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>catuṣpad</i> [<a href="#entry-pad_2" class="skt-link">pad_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-catu_spad" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-catu_spad" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-catu_spadii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>catuṣpadī</i> quadrupède — <a href="#entry-catu_spad" class="skt-link">n.</a> la gent quadrupède; (collectif) les animaux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catu_spada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>catuṣpada</i> [<a href="#entry-pada" class="skt-link">pada</a>] a. <a href="#entry-catu_spada" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-catu_spada" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-catu_spadaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>catuṣpadā</i> quadripartite <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (strophe) de 4 pieds [<a href="#entry-pada" class="skt-link">pada</a>]; consistant en 4 mots — <a href="#entry-catu_spada" class="skt-link">n.</a> astr. dernier demi-jour lunaire [<a href="#entry-kara_na" class="skt-link">karaṇa</a>] fixe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catu_spari">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>catuṣpari</i> [<a href="#entry-pari" class="skt-link">pari</a>] ind. manqué de quatre (not. au jeu <a href="#entry-pa_ncaka" class="skt-link">pañcaka</a>).<br> <a href="#entry-catur" class="skt-link">catur</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catu_spatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>catuṣpatha</i> [<a href="#entry-patha" class="skt-link">patha</a>] <a href="#entry-catu_spatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> intersection de deux routes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catur">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चतुर्</span> <i>catur</i> num. <a href="#entry-catur" class="skt-link">*</a> pl. quatre — adv. <i>catus</i> quatre fois — n. cf. <a href="#entry-catvaari" class="skt-link">catvāri</a> — f. cf. <a href="#entry-catas_r" class="skt-link">catasṛ</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>quatuor</i>; fr. quatre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चतुर</span> <i>catura</i> a. <a href="#entry-catura" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-catura" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caturaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caturā</i> habile, adroit; ingénieux; vif, rapide; charmant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturaadhyaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturādhyāya</i> a. <a href="#entry-caturaadhyaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caturaadhyaaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caturaadhyaayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caturādhyāyī</i> cf. <a href="#entry-caturadhyaaya" class="skt-link">caturadhyāya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturaadhyaayika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturādhyāyika</i> [<a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-caturaadhyaayika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caturaadhyaayikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caturādhyāyikā</i> id.<br> <a href="#entry-caturaadhyaaya" class="skt-link">caturādhyāya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturaanana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturānana</i> [<a href="#entry-aanana" class="skt-link">ānana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caturaanana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caturaanana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caturaananaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caturānanā</i> qui a quatre visages — <a href="#entry-caturaanana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Caturānana, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> «aux Quatre visages» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. représentation quadricéphale de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> avec les visages de <a href="#entry-Uvaraaha" class="skt-link">Varāha</a>, <a href="#entry-Unarasi_mha" class="skt-link">Narasiṃha</a>, <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> et <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-vaiku_n_thacaturmuurti" class="skt-link">vaikuṇṭhacaturmūrti</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturaazrama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturāśrama</i> [<a href="#entry-aazrama" class="skt-link">āśrama</a>] <a href="#entry-caturaazrama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caturaazrama" class="skt-link">n.</a> l'un des 4 stades de la vie brahmanique: <a href="#entry-brahmacarya" class="skt-link">brahmacarya</a>, <a href="#entry-g_rhastha" class="skt-link">gṛhastha</a>, <a href="#entry-vaanaprastha" class="skt-link">vānaprastha</a> et <a href="#entry-sannyaasa" class="skt-link">sannyāsa</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-puru_saartha" class="skt-link">puruṣārtha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturadhyaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturadhyāya</i> [<a href="#entry-adhyaaya" class="skt-link">adhyāya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caturadhyaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caturadhyaaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caturadhyaayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caturadhyāyī</i> constitué de 4 leçons — <a href="#entry-caturadhyaayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caturadhyāyī</i> gram. np. de la Caturadhyāyī ou Caturadhyāyikā ou Caturādhyāyikā de <a href="#entry-Uzaunaka_1" class="skt-link">Śaunaka_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturadhyaayika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturadhyāyika</i> [<a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-caturadhyaayika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caturadhyaayikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caturadhyāyikā</i> cf. <a href="#entry-caturadhyaayii" class="skt-link">caturadhyāyī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-caturadhyaaya" class="skt-link">caturadhyāya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturaṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-afga_1" class="skt-link">aṅga_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caturafga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caturafga" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caturafgaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caturaṅgā</i> formé de 4 parties; se dit not. d'une armée comportant des chars, des éléphants, des chevaux et des fantassins — <a href="#entry-caturafga" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<i>caturaṅgabala</i>] armée — <a href="#entry-caturafgaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caturaṅgā</i> [<i>caturaṅgābalā</i>] id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jeu d'échecs; ses pièces en sont: le roi [<a href="#entry-raajan" class="skt-link">rājan</a>], le ministre [<a href="#entry-mantrin" class="skt-link">mantrin</a>] (pour la dame), l'éléphant [<a href="#entry-hastin" class="skt-link">hastin</a>] (pour le fou), le cheval [<a href="#entry-azva" class="skt-link">aśva</a>] (pour le cavalier), le char [<a href="#entry-ratha" class="skt-link">ratha</a>] ou la barque [<a href="#entry-naukaa" class="skt-link">naukā</a>] (pour la tour) et le fantassin [<a href="#entry-padaatin" class="skt-link">padātin</a>] (pour le pion).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturafgaakrii_daa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturaṅgākrīḍā</i> [<a href="#entry-krii_daa" class="skt-link">krīḍā</a>] <a href="#entry-caturafgaakrii_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> partie d'échecs.<br> <a href="#entry-caturafga" class="skt-link">caturaṅga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturakṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-ak_sa" class="skt-link">akṣa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caturak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caturak_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caturak_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caturakṣī</i> [«qui a 4 yeux»] au regard perçant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. des chiens de <a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a>, ou d'<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> «qui voit tout».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturazra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturaśra</i> [<a href="#entry-azra_2" class="skt-link">aśra_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caturazra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caturazra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caturazraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caturaśrā</i> quadrangulaire; quadrilatère; rectangulaire, carré; régulier — <a href="#entry-caturazra" class="skt-link">m.</a> rectangle, carré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturazrataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturaśratā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-caturazrataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> régularité, harmonie; symétrie.<br> <a href="#entry-caturazra" class="skt-link">caturaśra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturazri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturaśri</i> [<a href="#entry-azri" class="skt-link">aśri</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caturazri" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caturazri" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caturazri" class="skt-link">f.</a> quandrangulaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturbaahu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturbāhu</i> [<a href="#entry-baahu" class="skt-link">bāhu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caturbaahu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caturbaahu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caturbaahu" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui a 4 bras (épith. de représentations de divinités).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturbhaa_nii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturbhāṇī</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaa_na" class="skt-link">bhāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-caturbhaa_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Caturbhāṇī, collection de 4 drames en style <a href="#entry-bhaa_na" class="skt-link">bhāṇa</a>, comprenant l'Ubhayābhisārika, le Padmaprābhṛtaka, le Dhūrtaviṭasaṃvāda et le <a href="#entry-Upaadataa_ditaka" class="skt-link">Pādatāḍitaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturbhuja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturbhuja</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuja" class="skt-link">bhuja</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caturbhuja" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caturbhuja" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caturbhujaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caturbhujā</i> qui a 4 bras (épith. de représentations de divinités).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturdazan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturdaśan</i> [<a href="#entry-dazan" class="skt-link">daśan</a>] num. <a href="#entry-caturdazan" class="skt-link">*</a> quatorze — <a href="#entry-caturdazii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caturdaśī</i> quatorzième jour de la quinzaine sombre du mois lunaire (nouvelle lune) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. il est de bonne augure, et jour de fête; cf. <a href="#entry-diipaavali" class="skt-link">dīpāvali</a> [<a href="#entry-vaiku_n_thacaturdazii" class="skt-link">vaikuṇṭhacaturdaśī</a>], <a href="#entry-n_rsi_mhacaturdazii" class="skt-link">nṛsiṃhacaturdaśī</a>, <a href="#entry-zivaraatri" class="skt-link">śivarātri</a>, <a href="#entry-anantavrata" class="skt-link">anantavrata</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturdhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturdhā</i> [<a href="#entry--dhaa" class="skt-link">-dhā</a>] adv. en 4 parties.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturdizam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturdiśam</i> [<a href="#entry-diz_2" class="skt-link">diś_2</a>] adv. dans les 4 directions, vers les 4 points cardinaux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturmaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturmāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-maasa" class="skt-link">māsa</a>] <a href="#entry-caturmaasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> période de 4 mois <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. période de sommeil de 4 mois de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>; elle se termine à la pleine lune [<a href="#entry-puur_nimaa" class="skt-link">pūrṇimā</a>] du mois <a href="#entry-kaarttika" class="skt-link">kārttika</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturmukha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturmukha</i> [<a href="#entry-mukha" class="skt-link">mukha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caturmukha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caturmukha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caturmukhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caturmukhī</i> qui a 4 faces — <a href="#entry-caturmukha" class="skt-link">m.</a> épith. de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturtha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturtha</i> [<a href="#entry--tha" class="skt-link">-tha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caturtha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caturtha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caturthii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caturthī</i> ord. quatrième — <a href="#entry-caturthii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caturthī</i> gram. datif (4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ déclinaison) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. quatrième jour du mois lunaire; <a href="#entry-ga_nezacaturthii" class="skt-link">gaṇeśacaturthī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturvar_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturvarṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-var_na" class="skt-link">varṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-caturvar_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. les 4 classes védiques.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturvarga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturvarga</i> [<a href="#entry-varga" class="skt-link">varga</a>] <a href="#entry-caturvarga" class="skt-link">m.</a> groupe de 4 objets <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. ouvrage en 4 parties.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturvargacintaama_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturvargacintāmaṇi</i> [<a href="#entry-cintaama_ni" class="skt-link">cintāmaṇi</a>] <a href="#entry-caturvargacintaama_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Caturvargacintāmaṇi «Trésor quadripartite», manuel de rituels [<a href="#entry-vrata" class="skt-link">vrata</a>] de <a href="#entry-Uhemaadri_1" class="skt-link">Hemādri_1</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-caturvar_na" class="skt-link">caturvarṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturvedin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturvedin</i> [<a href="#entry-vedin" class="skt-link">vedin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caturvedin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caturvedin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caturvedinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caturvedinī</i> soc. qui maîtrise les 4 Védas (forme des np. de brâhmanes); syn. <a href="#entry-caturvidya" class="skt-link">caturvidya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturvi_mzati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturviṃśati</i> [<a href="#entry-vi_mzati" class="skt-link">viṃśati</a>] num. <a href="#entry-caturvi_mzati" class="skt-link">f.</a> vingt-quatre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturvidya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturvidya</i> [<a href="#entry-vidyaa" class="skt-link">vidyā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-caturvidya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-caturvidya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-caturvidyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caturvidyā</i> soc. qui maîtrise les 4 Védas; syn. <a href="#entry-caturvedin" class="skt-link">caturvedin</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caturvyuuha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caturvyūha</i> [<a href="#entry-vyuuha" class="skt-link">vyūha</a>] <a href="#entry-caturvyuuha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. les 4 émanations divines de la tradition mystique <a href="#entry-vai_s_nava" class="skt-link">vaiṣṇava</a>: <a href="#entry-Uvaasudeva" class="skt-link">Vāsudeva</a>, <a href="#entry-Usafkar_sa_na" class="skt-link">Saṅkarṣaṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Upradyumna" class="skt-link">Pradyumna</a> et <a href="#entry-Uaniruddha" class="skt-link">Aniruddha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catuzcatvaari_mzat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>catuścatvāriṃśat</i> [<a href="#entry-catvaari_mzat" class="skt-link">catvāriṃśat</a>] <a href="#entry-catuzcatvaari_mzat" class="skt-link">f.</a> 44 — a. <a href="#entry-catuzcatvaari_mzata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-catuzcatvaari_mzata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <i>catuścatvāriṃśata</i> <a href="#entry-catuzcatvaari_mzatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>catuścatvāriṃśatī</i> 44⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> constitué de 44 parts (not. <a href="#entry-stoma" class="skt-link">stoma</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catvaaras">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चत्वारस्</span> <i>catvāras</i> cf. <a href="#entry-catur" class="skt-link">catur</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catvaari">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चत्वारि</span> <i>catvāri</i> [n. <a href="#entry-catur" class="skt-link">catur</a>] num. <a href="#entry-catvaari" class="skt-link">*</a> pl. quatre.<br> <i>catvāryāryasatyāni</i> bd. les Quatre Nobles Vérités [<a href="#entry-aaryasatya" class="skt-link">āryasatya</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catvaari_mzat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>catvāriṃśat</i> [<a href="#entry-dazat" class="skt-link">daśat</a>] <a href="#entry-catvaari_mzat" class="skt-link">f.</a> sg. quarante.<br> <a href="#entry-catvaari" class="skt-link">catvāri</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-catvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चत्वर</span> <i>catvara</i> [<a href="#entry-catur" class="skt-link">catur</a>] <a href="#entry-catvara" class="skt-link">n.</a> carrefour; place carrée; quartier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. carré de sol nivelé pour un sacrifice.<br> <i>dūrvācatvara</i> soc. carré de sol recouvert d'herbe <a href="#entry-duurvaa" class="skt-link">dūrvā</a> pour s'asseoir lors d'un sacrifice.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cau_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चौड</span> <i>cauḍa</i> var. <i>caula</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-cuu_daa" class="skt-link">cūḍā</a>] <a href="#entry-cau_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. <a href="#entry-sa_mskaara" class="skt-link">saṃskāra</a> de tonsure d'un enfant; cf. <a href="#entry-cuu_daakara_na" class="skt-link">cūḍākaraṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चौल</span> <i>caula</i> cf. <a href="#entry-cau_da" class="skt-link">cauḍa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caulukya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चौलुक्य</span> <i>caulukya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-culuka" class="skt-link">culuka</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-caulukya" class="skt-link">m.</a> np. de la dynastie Caulukya «Descendant du roi <a href="#entry-Uculuka" class="skt-link">Culuka</a>»; elle régnait au <a href="#entry-Ugurjara" class="skt-link">Gurjara</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukumaarapaala" class="skt-link">Kumārapāla</a>, <a href="#entry-Usiddharaaja" class="skt-link">Siddharāja</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चौर</span> <i>caura</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-cur" class="skt-link">cur</a>] <a href="#entry-caura" class="skt-link">m.</a> voleur, brigand; escroc, usurpateur, plagiaire — <a href="#entry-caurii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caurī</i> voleuse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cauraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चौरक</span> <i>cauraka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-cur" class="skt-link">cur</a>] <a href="#entry-cauraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> voleur — <a href="#entry-caurikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>caurikā</i> voleuse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caurapa_ncaazikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>caurapañcāśikā</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_ncaazikaa" class="skt-link">pañcāśikā</a>] <a href="#entry-caurapa_ncaazikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Caurapañcāśikā «50 stances du voleur», poème érotique de <a href="#entry-Ubilha_na" class="skt-link">Bilhaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cauravidyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cauravidyā</i> [<a href="#entry-vidyaa" class="skt-link">vidyā</a>] <a href="#entry-cauravidyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> science du vol, l'un des 64 arts [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cauraviira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cauravīra</i> [<a href="#entry-viira" class="skt-link">vīra</a>] <a href="#entry-cauraviira" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. <i>cauravīrās</i> pègre.<br> <a href="#entry-caura" class="skt-link">caura</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caurya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चौर्य</span> <i>caurya</i> [<a href="#entry-caura" class="skt-link">caura</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-caurya" class="skt-link">n.</a> tricherie, vol.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cauryazaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cauryaśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-cauryazaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> science du vol; cf. <a href="#entry-U_sa_nmukhakalpa" class="skt-link">Ṣaṇmukhakalpa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-caurya" class="skt-link">caurya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cavarga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चवर्ग</span> <i>cavarga</i> [<i>(ca)</i>-<a href="#entry-varga" class="skt-link">varga</a>] <a href="#entry-cavarga" class="skt-link">m.</a> phon. la classe des palatales.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-caya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चय</span> <i>caya</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ci" class="skt-link">ci</a>] <a href="#entry-caya" class="skt-link">m.</a> tas, amas, masse; gerbe, bouquet, collection, assemblage; multitude <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tas de terre; terrassement, fondation, mur; fortification, glacis.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-cayana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चयन</span> <i>cayana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ci" class="skt-link">ci</a>] <a href="#entry-cayana" class="skt-link">n.</a> pile de bois, bûcher <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> autel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_nc">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">चञ्च्</span> <i>cañc</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(cañcati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>cañcita</i><i>)</i> sauter; bouger, pendre, vaciller, être instable; trembler, palpiter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_nca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चञ्च</span> <i>cañca</i> <a href="#entry-ca_nca" class="skt-link">m.</a> panier d'osier — <a href="#entry-ca_ncaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cañcā</i> objet d'osier ou de canne; panier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_ncala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चञ्चल</span> <i>cañcala</i> [int. <a href="#entry-cal" class="skt-link">cal</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ca_ncala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ca_ncala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ca_ncalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cañcalā</i> agité; instable, inconstant; fluctuant; tremblant, vacillant — <a href="#entry-ca_ncala" class="skt-link">m.</a> vent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> libertin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_ncalataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>cañcalatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-ca_ncalataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> instabilité, inconstance.<br> <a href="#entry-ca_ncala" class="skt-link">cañcala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_ncita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चञ्चित</span> <i>cañcita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ca_nc" class="skt-link">cañc</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ca_ncita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ca_ncita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ca_ncitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>cañcitā</i> tremblant, palpitant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ca_ncu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चञ्चु</span> <i>cañcu</i> a. <a href="#entry-ca_ncu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ca_ncu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ca_ncu" class="skt-link">f.</a> ifc. fameux pour — <a href="#entry-ca_ncu" class="skt-link">m.</a> daim — <a href="#entry-ca_ncu" class="skt-link">f.</a> bec d'oiseau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ce_s_t">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">चेष्ट्</span> <i>ceṣṭ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ceṣṭati)</i> pr. md. <i>(ceṣṭate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ceṣṭita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>ceṣṭavya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> gesticuler, remuer, s'agiter — ca. <i>(ceṣṭayati)</i> mettre en branle, exciter; exhorter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ce_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चेष्ट</span> <i>ceṣṭa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ce_s_t" class="skt-link">ceṣṭ</a>] <a href="#entry-ce_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> sorte de poisson — <a href="#entry-ce_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ce_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ceṣṭā</i> geste; mouvement, effort, activité; exécution, conduite <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>gestus</i>; ang. gesture; fr. geste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ce_s_taka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
